import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { copyFixture } from "../../test/helpers/fixture";
import { stubSummarizer } from "../../test/helpers/summarizer";
import { makeTempDir } from "../../test/helpers/temp";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { acquireSummaryLock, releaseSummaryLock } from "../db/store/lock";
import { requireRepository } from "../db/store/structure";
import { loadSummaries } from "../db/store/summaries";
import { countEmbedded } from "../db/store/summary-vectors";
import { describe as describeTarget } from "../engine/describe";
import { SummariesOffError } from "../engine/errors";
import { embedSummaries, type EmbedFn } from "../index/embed";
import { repoKey } from "../index/paths";
import { indexRepository } from "../index/pipeline";
import { runSummaries } from "../index/summarize/run";
import type { RepoWatcher } from "../index/watch";
import { createBackgroundService, MAX_AUTO_REFRESH_FILES } from "./service";

const fakeEmbed: EmbedFn = async (texts) => texts.map(() => Float32Array.from([1, 0, 0]));

// Date stays real: lock staleness and stage times are compared against the clock
beforeEach(() => { vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ["setTimeout", "clearTimeout", "setInterval", "clearInterval"] }); });
afterEach(() => { vi.useRealTimers(); });

/** A watcher the test drives: `changed(paths)` is what chokidar would report after its debounce. */
function fakeWatcher(){
    let onChanges: ((paths: string[]) => void) | null = null;
    let closed = false;
    const watch = (_root: string, callback: (paths: string[]) => void): RepoWatcher => {
        onChanges = callback;
        return { ready: Promise.resolve(), async close(){ closed = true; } };
    };
    return { watch, changed: (paths: string[]) => onChanges!(paths), isClosed: () => closed };
}

/** The fixture summarized and embedded throughout, as the CLI leaves VS Code. */
async function summarizedFixture(): Promise<string> {
    const root = copyFixture();
    indexRepository(root);
    await runSummaries(root, { summarizer: stubSummarizer().summarizer, transport: "direct" });
    await embedSummaries(root, { embed: fakeEmbed });
    return root;
}

function rowStatus(root: string, targetPath: string){
    const db = getDb();
    return loadSummaries(db, requireRepository(db, repoKey(root))).find((s) => s.path === targetPath)?.status;
}

function embedded(root: string): number {
    const db = getDb();
    return countEmbedded(db, requireRepository(db, repoKey(root)));
}

describe("background service", () => {
    it("a saved file reaches the index at once, and its summary after 60 quiet seconds", async () => {
        const root = await summarizedFixture();
        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const stub = stubSummarizer();
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: stub.summarizer, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed });
        await service.setRepo(root);
        await service.idle();

        fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, "src/core/log.ts"), "export const extra = 1;\n");
        watcher.changed(["src/core/log.ts"]);
        await service.idle();
        expect(describeTarget(root, "src/core/log.ts").symbols?.map((s) => s.name)).toContain("extra");
        expect(rowStatus(root, "src/core/log.ts")).toBe("stale");

        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(59_000);
        await service.idle();
        expect(stub.requests).toHaveLength(0);

        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
        await service.idle();
        expect(stub.subjects()).toEqual(["File: src/core/log.ts"]);
        expect(rowStatus(root, "src/core/log.ts")).toBe("fresh");
        expect(rowStatus(root, "src/core")).toBe("stale");   // its folder waits to be opened or swept
        expect(embedded(root)).toBe(13);                     // the new summary has its vector again
        await service.stop();
    });

    it("another save within the minute restarts that file's wait", async () => {
        const root = await summarizedFixture();
        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const stub = stubSummarizer();
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: stub.summarizer, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed });
        await service.setRepo(root);

        fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, "src/core/log.ts"), "export const one = 1;\n");
        watcher.changed(["src/core/log.ts"]);
        await service.idle();
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(40_000);
        fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, "src/core/log.ts"), "export const two = 2;\n");
        watcher.changed(["src/core/log.ts"]);
        await service.idle();

        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(59_000);   // 99 s after the first save, 59 s after the second
        await service.idle();
        expect(stub.requests).toHaveLength(0);
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(1_000);
        await service.idle();
        expect(stub.requests).toHaveLength(1);
        await service.stop();
    });

    it("refreshes at most 50 changed files at once, so a big pull can't spend silently", async () => {
        const root = makeTempDir("neb-many-");
        fs.mkdirSync(path.join(root, "src"));
        const names = Array.from({ length: 52 }, (_, i) => `src/f${String(i).padStart(2, "0")}.ts`);
        for (const name of names) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(root, name), "export const v = 1;\n");
        indexRepository(root);
        await runSummaries(root, { summarizer: stubSummarizer().summarizer, transport: "direct" });

        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const stub = stubSummarizer();
        const logs: string[] = [];
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: stub.summarizer, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed, log: (m) => logs.push(m) });
        await service.setRepo(root);
        for (const name of names) fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, name), "export const w = 2;\n");
        watcher.changed(names);
        await service.idle();
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000);
        await service.idle();

        expect(stub.requests).toHaveLength(MAX_AUTO_REFRESH_FILES);
        expect(logs).toContain("refreshed at most 50 of 52 changed files; the rest stay out of date until opened or npm run summarize");
        await service.stop();
    });

    it("re-indexes the whole repo when more than 500 paths change at once", async () => {
        const root = await summarizedFixture();
        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const logs: string[] = [];
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: null, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed, log: (m) => logs.push(m) });
        await service.setRepo(root);
        watcher.changed(Array.from({ length: 501 }, (_, i) => `gen/f${i}.ts`));
        await service.idle();
        expect(logs).toContain("re-indexing the whole repo (501 paths changed)");
        await service.stop();
    });

    it("summarize now needs a key, and queues the new summary's embedding", async () => {
        const root = await summarizedFixture();
        const off = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: null, watch: fakeWatcher().watch, embed: fakeEmbed });
        await off.setRepo(root);
        await expect(off.summarizeNow("src/core/log.ts")).rejects.toThrow(SummariesOffError);
        await off.stop();

        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: stubSummarizer().summarizer, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed });
        await service.setRepo(root);
        fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, "src/core/log.ts"), "export const extra = 1;\n");
        watcher.changed(["src/core/log.ts"]);
        await service.idle();

        expect(await service.summarizeNow("src/core/log.ts")).toMatchObject({ status: "fresh" });
        await service.idle();
        expect(embedded(root)).toBe(13);
        await service.stop();
    });

    it("waits while another summary run holds the lock, then tries again", async () => {
        const root = await summarizedFixture();
        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const stub = stubSummarizer();
        const logs: string[] = [];
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: stub.summarizer, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed, log: (m) => logs.push(m) });
        await service.setRepo(root);
        const db = getDb();

        acquireSummaryLock(db);
        try{
            fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, "src/core/log.ts"), "export const extra = 1;\n");
            watcher.changed(["src/core/log.ts"]);
            await service.idle();
            await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000);
            await service.idle();
            expect(stub.requests).toHaveLength(0);
            expect(logs.some((m) => m.endsWith("trying again in 30 s"))).toBe(true);
        } finally{
            releaseSummaryLock(db);
        }

        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(30_000);
        await service.idle();
        expect(stub.requests).toHaveLength(1);
        await service.stop();
    });

    it("sweeps out-of-date folders every 10 minutes, deepest first, up to the repo", async () => {
        const root = await summarizedFixture();
        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const stub = stubSummarizer();
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: stub.summarizer, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed });
        await service.setRepo(root);
        fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, "src/core/log.ts"), "export const extra = 1;\n");
        watcher.changed(["src/core/log.ts"]);
        await service.idle();
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000);
        await service.idle();
        expect(rowStatus(root, "src/core")).toBe("stale");

        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(9 * 60_000);   // 10 minutes after setRepo
        await service.idle();
        // the repo's prompt names the root folder, which buildFolders calls by the copy's own directory name
        expect(stub.subjects()).toEqual(["File: src/core/log.ts", "Folder: src/core", "Folder: src", `Repository: ${path.basename(root)}`]);
        expect(rowStatus(root, "src/core")).toBe("fresh");
        await service.stop();
    });

    it("stops watching and forgets pending waits when the repo is cleared", async () => {
        const root = await summarizedFixture();
        const watcher = fakeWatcher();
        const stub = stubSummarizer();
        const service = createBackgroundService({ summarizer: stub.summarizer, watch: watcher.watch, embed: fakeEmbed });
        await service.setRepo(root);
        fs.appendFileSync(path.join(root, "src/core/log.ts"), "export const extra = 1;\n");
        watcher.changed(["src/core/log.ts"]);
        await service.idle();

        await service.setRepo(null);
        expect(watcher.isClosed()).toBe(true);
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000);
        await service.idle();
        expect(stub.requests).toHaveLength(0);
    });
});
```
  Why the sweep test expects those four subjects:
  - **First the 60 s refresh** sends `log.ts`.
  - **Then the sweep** finds `src/core` out of date with its children settled, and sends it.
  - **That turns `src` stale.** The sweep plans with `refreshStaleFolders: true`, so `src` goes out next, then the repo.
  - **That's 3 folder requests**, under the sweep's limit of 20.
  - **The repo's subject is `Repository: <the copy's folder name>`.** The copy is indexed under the name `buildFolders` gives it, its temp directory's basename, and that's the name `renderRepoPrompt` writes on the first line.

/** How many folder and repo summaries are stale: what the background worker's sweep has to do. */
export function countStaleFolders(db: Db, repoId: number): number {
    return db.select({ n: sql<number>`count(*)` }).from(summaries)
        .where(and(eq(summaries.repoId, repoId), eq(summaries.status, "stale"), sql`${summaries.targetKind} != 'file'`)).get()?.n ?? 0;
}

- [ ] **Step 5: The service** — `lib/background/service.ts`:
```ts
import path from "path";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { IndexLockedError } from "../db/store/lock";
import { findRepository } from "../db/store/structure";
import { countStaleFolders, targetKey, type StoredSummary } from "../db/store/summaries";
import { NoActiveRepoError, SummariesOffError } from "../engine/errors";
import { embedSummaries, type EmbedFn } from "../index/embed";
import { repoKey } from "../index/paths";
import { indexRepository, type IndexOptions, type IndexResult } from "../index/pipeline";
import { DEFAULT_BUDGET_USD, runSummaries, StructureNotReadyError, summarizeNow } from "../index/summarize/run";
import { refreshSummaryStatus } from "../index/summarize/status";
import type { Summarizer } from "../index/summarize/types";
import { applyFileChanges } from "../index/update";
import { watchRepository, type RepoWatcher } from "../index/watch";

/** More changed paths than this at once (a branch switch, a big pull), and one full run is cheaper than an update. */
export const MAX_INCREMENTAL_PATHS = 500;
/** At most this many file summaries per refresh, so a pull that touches thousands of files can't spend silently (ADR-038). */
export const MAX_AUTO_REFRESH_FILES = 50;
/** At most this many out-of-date folders per sweep, deepest first; their parents follow in later sweeps. */
export const SWEEP_MAX_FOLDERS = 20;
/** Summaries embedded per slice: about 3.5 s of MiniLM at its measured 18 a second, so no other job waits long. */
export const EMBED_SLICE = 64;

export type ServiceTimings = {
    /** A file's summary is redone this long after its last change (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.6). */
    summaryQuietMs: number;
    /** How often out-of-date folders are swept. */
    sweepEveryMs: number;
    /** How long to wait before retrying work another process had locked. */
    retryLockedMs: number;
};

export const DEFAULT_TIMINGS: ServiceTimings = { summaryQuietMs: 60_000, sweepEveryMs: 10 * 60_000, retryLockedMs: 30_000 };

export type BackgroundDeps = {
    /** null without an API key: structure updates still run, summaries don't. */
    summarizer: Summarizer | null;
    /** The file watcher; tests pass a fake. */
    watch?: (repoRoot: string, onChanges: (paths: string[]) => void) => RepoWatcher;
    /** The embedding model; tests pass a fake. */
    embed?: EmbedFn;
    log?: (message: string) => void;
    timings?: Partial<ServiceTimings>;
};

export type BackgroundService = {
    /** Points the worker at a repo (null: none): catches up on what changed while nothing watched, then watches it. */
    setRepo(repoRoot: string | null): Promise<void>;
    /** "Summarize now" for the Map (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.3). It runs at once, beside the queued maintenance work. */
    summarizeNow(targetPath: string): Promise<StoredSummary>;
    /** A full structure run (POST /api/index), queued behind the work already waiting. */
    indexNow(onProgress?: IndexOptions["onProgress"]): Promise<IndexResult>;
    /** Resolves once every queued job has run (tests use it). */
    idle(): Promise<void>;
    stop(): Promise<void>;
};

/**
 * The background worker's logic (ADR-038), with no process or IPC in it: Task 13's scripts/background.ts runs it. It owns
 * every write while the Map is open:
 * - watched paths become incremental structure updates (ADR-037);
 * - each changed file's summary is redone 60 quiet seconds later, at most 50 at once;
 * - out-of-date folders are swept every 10 minutes;
 * - whatever lacks a vector is embedded in slices.
 * That maintenance work runs one job at a time. "Summarize now" doesn't wait for it.
 */
export function createBackgroundService(deps: BackgroundDeps): BackgroundService {
    const timings = { ...DEFAULT_TIMINGS, ...deps.timings };
    const log = deps.log ?? (() => {});
    const watchFn = deps.watch ?? watchRepository;
    const summarizerAvailable = deps.summarizer !== null;

    let root: string | null = null;
    let watcher: RepoWatcher | null = null;
    let sweepTimer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
    const summaryTimers = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
    const retryTimers = new Set<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();
    const pendingUpdate = new Set<string>();
    const pendingRefresh = new Set<string>();
    let embedQueued = false;
    let chain: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();

    /** Maintenance runs one job at a time, in the order queued. A failed job is logged and the next one still runs. */
    function exclusive<T>(job: () => T | Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
        const run = chain.then(job);
        chain = run.catch((err: unknown) => log(`job failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`));
        return run;
    }

    function later(fn: () => void, ms: number): void {
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            retryTimers.delete(timer);
            fn();
        }, ms);
        retryTimers.add(timer);
    }

    // ---- structure ------------------------------------------------------------------
    function queueUpdate(paths: readonly string[]): void {
        const wasEmpty = pendingUpdate.size === 0;
        for (const p of paths) pendingUpdate.add(p);
        if (wasEmpty) void exclusive(runUpdate);   // one job takes everything queued by the time it starts
    }

    function runUpdate(): void {
        const repo = root;
        const paths = [...pendingUpdate].sort();
        pendingUpdate.clear();
        if (repo === null || paths.length === 0) return;

        try{
            if (paths.length > MAX_INCREMENTAL_PATHS || paths.some((p) => p === ".gitignore" || p.endsWith("/.gitignore"))){
                log(`re-indexing the whole repo (${paths.length} paths changed)`);
                indexRepository(repo);
                // too many to refresh one by one: they stay out of date until opened, swept, or npm run summarize
            } else{
                const result = applyFileChanges(repo, paths);
                log(`updated ${result.changed.length} changed and ${result.deleted.length} deleted files in ${Math.round(result.totalMs)} ms`);
                for (const p of result.changed) armSummaryTimer(p);
                for (const p of result.deleted) cancelSummaryTimer(p);
            }
            refreshSummaryStatus(repo, { summarizerAvailable });
        } catch (err){
            if (!(err instanceof IndexLockedError)) throw err;
            log(`another index run holds the lock; trying again in ${timings.retryLockedMs / 1000} s`);
            later(() => queueUpdate(paths), timings.retryLockedMs);
        }
    }

    // ---- summaries --------------------------------------------------------------------
    function armSummaryTimer(filePath: string, delayMs: number = timings.summaryQuietMs): void {
        cancelSummaryTimer(filePath);
        summaryTimers.set(filePath, setTimeout(() => {
            summaryTimers.delete(filePath);
            queueRefresh([filePath]);
        }, delayMs));
    }

    function cancelSummaryTimer(filePath: string): void {
        const timer = summaryTimers.get(filePath);
        if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
        summaryTimers.delete(filePath);
    }

    function queueRefresh(paths: readonly string[]): void {
        const wasEmpty = pendingRefresh.size === 0;
        for (const p of paths) pendingRefresh.add(p);
        if (wasEmpty) void exclusive(runRefresh);
    }

    /**
     * Redoes the summaries of files whose quiet minute is up (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.6): most-imported first, at most
     * MAX_AUTO_REFRESH_FILES, through the regular API, under the repo's spend cap (ADR-035). Their folders turn stale
     * and wait to be opened or swept, so a pull can't set off hundreds of Sonnet calls.
     */
    async function runRefresh(): Promise<void> {
        const repo = root;
        const paths = [...pendingRefresh].sort();
        pendingRefresh.clear();
        if (repo === null || paths.length === 0 || deps.summarizer === null) return;

        const wanted = new Set(paths.map((p) => targetKey("file", p)));
        try{
            const result = await runSummaries(repo, {
                summarizer: deps.summarizer,
                transport: "direct",
                refreshStaleFolders: false,
                wait: false,
                budgetUsd: DEFAULT_BUDGET_USD,
                select: (target) => wanted.has(targetKey(target.kind, target.path)),
                maxTargets: MAX_AUTO_REFRESH_FILES,
                onProgress: (message) => log(`summaries: ${message}`),
            });
            if (paths.length > MAX_AUTO_REFRESH_FILES){
                log(`refreshed at most ${MAX_AUTO_REFRESH_FILES} of ${paths.length} changed files; the rest stay out of date until opened or npm run summarize`);
            }
            if (result.summarized > 0) queueEmbed();
        } catch (err){
            if (!(err instanceof IndexLockedError) && !(err instanceof StructureNotReadyError)) throw err;
            log(`summaries: busy (another summary run, or a structure run); trying again in ${timings.retryLockedMs / 1000} s`);
            for (const p of paths) armSummaryTimer(p, timings.retryLockedMs);
        }
    }

    /** Out-of-date folders whose children are settled, deepest first, at most SWEEP_MAX_FOLDERS (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.6). */
    async function runSweep(): Promise<void> {
        const repo = root;
        if (repo === null || deps.summarizer === null) return;
        const db = getDb();
        const repoId = findRepository(db, repoKey(repo));
        if (repoId === null || countStaleFolders(db, repoId) === 0) return;

        try{
            const result = await runSummaries(repo, {
                summarizer: deps.summarizer,
                transport: "direct",
                refreshStaleFolders: true,
                wait: false,
                budgetUsd: DEFAULT_BUDGET_USD,
                select: (target) => target.kind !== "file",
                maxTargets: SWEEP_MAX_FOLDERS,
                onProgress: (message) => log(`sweep: ${message}`),
            });
            // the run planned with stale folders as work; between runs they read as lazy again (ADR-032)
            refreshSummaryStatus(repo, { summarizerAvailable });
            if (result.summarized > 0) queueEmbed();
        } catch (err){
            if (!(err instanceof IndexLockedError) && !(err instanceof StructureNotReadyError)) throw err;
            log("sweep: busy; trying again at the next sweep");
        }
    }

    // ---- embeddings -------------------------------------------------------------------
    function queueEmbed(): void {
        if (embedQueued) return;
        embedQueued = true;
        void exclusive(runEmbed);
    }

    async function runEmbed(): Promise<void> {
        embedQueued = false;
        const repo = root;
        if (repo === null) return;
        const result = await embedSummaries(repo, { embed: deps.embed, limit: EMBED_SLICE });
        if (result.remaining > 0) queueEmbed();   // one slice per job, so updates queued meanwhile go first
    }

    // ---- lifecycle --------------------------------------------------------------------
    async function stopWatching(): Promise<void> {
        if (sweepTimer) clearInterval(sweepTimer);
        sweepTimer = null;
        for (const timer of summaryTimers.values()) clearTimeout(timer);
        summaryTimers.clear();
        for (const timer of retryTimers) clearTimeout(timer);
        retryTimers.clear();
        pendingUpdate.clear();
        pendingRefresh.clear();
        const current = watcher;
        watcher = null;
        await current?.close();
    }

    async function idle(): Promise<void> {
        for (;;){
            const current = chain;
            await current;
            if (current === chain) return;
        }
    }

    return {
        async setRepo(next){
            await stopWatching();
            root = next === null ? null : path.resolve(next);
            const repo = root;
            if (repo === null) return;

            // the startup check (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.6): one warm full run catches what changed while nothing watched
            const indexed = await exclusive(() => {
                if (findRepository(getDb(), repoKey(repo)) === null){
                    log(`${repo} isn't indexed yet; run npm run index -- "${repo}"`);
                    return false;
                }
                try{
                    indexRepository(repo);
                } catch (err){
                    if (!(err instanceof IndexLockedError)) throw err;
                    log("another index run holds the lock; watching without the startup check");
                }
                refreshSummaryStatus(repo, { summarizerAvailable });
                return true;
            });
            if (!indexed || root !== repo) return;   // never indexed, or switched again meanwhile

            watcher = watchFn(repo, queueUpdate);
            sweepTimer = setInterval(() => { void exclusive(runSweep); }, timings.sweepEveryMs);
            queueEmbed();   // summaries the CLI wrote but never embedded (an interrupted run) get their vectors here
            log(`watching ${repo}`);
        },

        async summarizeNow(targetPath){
            if (deps.summarizer === null) throw new SummariesOffError();
            if (root === null) throw new NoActiveRepoError();
            const row = await summarizeNow(root, targetPath, deps.summarizer);
            queueEmbed();
            return row;
        },

        indexNow(onProgress){
            const repo = root;
            if (repo === null) return Promise.reject(new NoActiveRepoError());
            return exclusive(() => {
                const result = indexRepository(repo, { onProgress });
                refreshSummaryStatus(repo, { summarizerAvailable });
                return result;
            });
        },

        idle,

        async stop(){
            await stopWatching();
            root = null;
            await idle();
        },
    };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Run them to make sure they pass** — `npx vitest run lib/index/summarize/run.test.ts lib/background/service.test.ts`. Expected: PASS (2 new run tests, 8 service tests).

- [ ] **Step 7: Document it.**
  1. Append **ADR-038** to `docs/ENGINEERING_DECISIONS.md`:
```md

## ADR-038 — A background worker process owns the index while the Map is open (‹today›)

**Context:** PIVOT_SPEC.md §3 has the Next server run "background indexing on a worker thread". §5.6 asks for a watcher, debounced structure updates, a summary refresh 60 s after a file's last change, a periodic sweep of out-of-date folders, and re-embedding. On VS Code a warm full run takes 4.9 s and planning the summaries about 0.2 s. On the server's event loop, either would stall every request behind it, and S3 allows 100 ms per interaction. The server is bundled by Turbopack, so a worker thread would have to load the TypeScript engine through the bundle, or through a loader that isn't known to apply inside threads.
**Decision:**
- **A forked child process, not a worker thread.** The server forks `scripts/background.ts` through tsx (`--import tsx`, resolved from the checkout), the way every CLI script runs. The child owns every write while the Map is open. The server stays a reader: level, describe, search and status read the shared SQLite file (WAL). "Summarize now" and "re-index" go to the child as requests over the IPC channel.
- **One job at a time.** Structure updates, summary refreshes, sweeps and embedding slices run one after another. "Summarize now" runs at once beside them, deduplicated per target (ADR-033, amended).
- **Structure.** A batch of watched paths goes through `applyFileChanges` (ADR-037). Over 500 paths, or a changed `.gitignore`, gets one full run instead. Every update ends by re-deriving the summaries stage (ADR-032, amended).
- **Summaries.** Each changed file gets a 60 s timer, which another save restarts. Due files are refreshed through the regular API: most-imported first, at most 50 per refresh, under the cumulative $15 cap (ADR-035). Their folders turn stale and wait.
- **The sweep.** Every 10 minutes it redoes out-of-date folders whose children are settled, deepest first, at most 20. A pull can't set off hundreds of Sonnet calls at once.
- **Embeddings.** Whatever lacks a vector is embedded in slices of 64, so a structure update never waits more than a few seconds.
- **Startup.** On start and on every repo switch, one warm full run catches what changed while nothing watched (4.9 s on VS Code, in the child). Size-and-mtime filtering (§5.6) waits until that grows.
- **Locks.** Work another process holds a lock on is retried after 30 s.
- **Crashes.** The server's supervisor restarts a crashed child after growing delays (1, 2, 5, 10, then 30 s) and answers anything it owed with an error. The child exits when the server does, because its IPC channel closes.
**Alternatives:**
- (a) Everything on the server's event loop: simplest, but a save on VS Code would freeze the Map for up to a second, and a branch switch for tens of seconds.
- (b) A worker thread, as the spec first said: loading TypeScript inside one under Turbopack is unproven, and a crash in a thread can take the server with it.
- (c) Refresh every changed file however many there are: a branch switch could send thousands of requests silently.
**Consequences:**
- The Map never waits on indexing.
- A second Node process costs about 100–200 MB.
- The child needs tsx at run time, a devDependency: fine for a local tool, and noted for the desktop build.
- When more than 50 files change at once, the rest keep stale summaries until opened, swept, or summarized by the CLI.
**Revisit when:** the desktop app (Electron or Tauri) replaces Next as the host; the worker becomes its background process.
```
  2. **`docs/PIVOT_SPEC.md` §5.6:**
     - **Watcher.** The bullet becomes: "While the Next server runs, its background worker (ADR-038) watches the repo root with chokidar, ignoring `.git`, `node_modules` and the other folders discovery skips. Structure updates are debounced by 1 s (5 s at most during a steady stream). /* ─────────────────────────── Indexing (SSE) ──────────────────────── */

/** The retired Phase 2 pipeline's phases: only lib/index-repo.ts uses it, and both go in V1.3 (Task 21). */
export type IndexPhase = "scanning" | "chunking" | "embedding";

/** The structure stage's steps, in order (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.2). */
export type IndexStage = "discover" | "parse" | "write" | "resolve" | "aggregate";

/** POST /api/index SSE events — docs/API_CONTRACTS.md §4 */
export type IndexEventMap = {
  progress: { stage: IndexStage; done: number; total: number };
  done: {
    files: number;
    changed: number;
    deleted: number;
    edges: number;
    folders: number;
    levelEdges: number;
    /** Per-file failures: indexing is per-file error-tolerant (ADR-021). */
    errors: Array<{ path: string; error: string }>;
    /** Files left out (over 1 MB, unreadable). */
    skipped: number;
    totalMs: number;
  };
  error: { code: ErrorCode; message: string };
};

export type IndexEvent = {
  [K in keyof IndexEventMap]: { event: K; data: IndexEventMap[K] };
}[keyof IndexEventMap];
```

- [ ] **Step 2: The protocol** — `lib/background/protocol.ts`:
```ts
import type { ErrorEnvelope, IndexEventMap, SummarizeResponse } from "../contracts";

/** Server → worker over the IPC channel (ADR-038). Requests carry an id, and the answer carries it back. */
export type ParentMessage =
    | { type: "repo"; root: string | null }
    | { type: "summarize"; id: number; path: string }
    | { type: "index"; id: number };

/** A request that couldn't be done, with the code and message the API shows. */
export type ApiFailure = { ok: false; error: ErrorEnvelope["error"] };
export type SummarizeOutcome = { ok: true; summary: SummarizeResponse } | ApiFailure;
export type IndexOutcome = { ok: true; result: IndexEventMap["done"] } | ApiFailure;

/** Worker → server. "ready" follows each "repo" message: the worker is up and watching. */
export type ChildMessage =
    | { type: "ready" }
    | { type: "summarized"; id: number; outcome: SummarizeOutcome }
    | { type: "index-progress"; id: number; progress: IndexEventMap["progress"] }
    | { type: "indexed"; id: number; outcome: IndexOutcome };
```

  2. `lib/background/supervisor.test.ts`:
```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { ChildMessage, ParentMessage } from "./protocol";
import { createSupervisor, type ChildLike } from "./supervisor";

class FakeChild implements ChildLike {
    sent: ParentMessage[] = [];
    killed = false;
    private messageListeners: ((message: ChildMessage) => void)[] = [];
    private exitListeners: ((code: number | null) => void)[] = [];
    send(message: ParentMessage): boolean { this.sent.push(message); return true; }
    onMessage(listener: (message: ChildMessage) => void): void { this.messageListeners.push(listener); }
    onExit(listener: (code: number | null) => void): void { this.exitListeners.push(listener); }
    kill(): void { this.killed = true; }
    reply(message: ChildMessage): void { for (const listener of this.messageListeners) listener(message); }
    exit(code: number | null): void { for (const listener of this.exitListeners) listener(code); }
}

function supervised(){
    const children: FakeChild[] = [];
    const supervisor = createSupervisor(() => {
        const child = new FakeChild();
        children.push(child);
        return child;
    }, { log: () => {} });
    return { supervisor, children };
}

const summary = { path: "src/a.ts", kind: "file" as const, tagline: "A", summary: "S.", summaryStatus: "fresh" as const };

beforeEach(() => { vi.useFakeTimers(); });
afterEach(() => { vi.useRealTimers(); });

describe("supervisor", () => {
    it("starts the worker on the first setRepo, tells it the repo, and tells the same worker about the next one", () => {
        const { supervisor, children } = supervised();
        expect(children).toHaveLength(0);
        supervisor.setRepo("/r");
        expect(children).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(children[0].sent).toEqual([{ type: "repo", root: "/r" }]);
        supervisor.setRepo("/s");
        expect(children).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(children[0].sent.at(-1)).toEqual({ type: "repo", root: "/s" });
    });

    it("answers a summarize request with the worker's reply", async () => {
        const { supervisor, children } = supervised();
        supervisor.setRepo("/r");
        const answer = supervisor.summarize("src/a.ts");
        expect(children[0].sent.at(-1)).toEqual({ type: "summarize", id: 1, path: "src/a.ts" });
        children[0].reply({ type: "summarized", id: 1, outcome: { ok: true, summary } });
        await expect(answer).resolves.toEqual({ ok: true, summary });
    });

    it("answers what a dying worker owed with an error, and brings a new one back with the repo after 1 s", async () => {
        const { supervisor, children } = supervised();
        supervisor.setRepo("/r");
        const answer = supervisor.summarize("src/a.ts");
        children[0].exit(1);
        await expect(answer).resolves.toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: { code: "INTERNAL" } });

        vi.advanceTimersByTime(999);
        expect(children).toHaveLength(1);
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);
        expect(children).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(children[1].sent).toEqual([{ type: "repo", root: "/r" }]);
    });

    it("fails a request at once while no worker is running, instead of waiting", async () => {
        const { supervisor, children } = supervised();
        supervisor.setRepo("/r");
        children[0].exit(1);
        await expect(supervisor.summarize("src/a.ts")).resolves.toMatchObject({ ok: false, error: { code: "INTERNAL" } });
    });

    it("waits longer after each crash, and starts over once a worker reports ready", () => {
        const { supervisor, children } = supervised();
        supervisor.setRepo("/r");
        children[0].exit(1);
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(1_000);      // the first restart comes after 1 s
        children[1].exit(1);
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(1_999);
        expect(children).toHaveLength(2);
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(1);          // the second after 2 s
        expect(children).toHaveLength(3);
        children[2].reply({ type: "ready" });
        children[2].exit(1);
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(1_000);      // a worker that came up resets the wait
        expect(children).toHaveLength(4);
    });

    it("forwards a re-index's progress, then its result", async () => {
        const { supervisor, children } = supervised();
        supervisor.setRepo("/r");
        const progress: unknown[] = [];
        const answer = supervisor.index((p) => progress.push(p));
        const { id } = children[0].sent.at(-1) as { id: number };
        children[0].reply({ type: "index-progress", id, progress: { stage: "parse", done: 1, total: 2 } });
        const result = { files: 2, changed: 1, deleted: 0, edges: 1, folders: 1, levelEdges: 1, errors: [], skipped: 0, totalMs: 5 };
        children[0].reply({ type: "indexed", id, outcome: { ok: true, result } });
        await expect(answer).resolves.toEqual({ ok: true, result });
        expect(progress).toEqual([{ stage: "parse", done: 1, total: 2 }]);
    });

    it("stop kills the worker and never restarts it", () => {
        const { supervisor, children } = supervised();
        supervisor.setRepo("/r");
        supervisor.stop();
        expect(children[0].killed).toBe(true);
        children[0].exit(null);
        vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000);
        expect(children).toHaveLength(1);
    });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The child's message handling** — `lib/background/child.ts`:
```ts
import type { IndexEventMap, SummarizeResponse } from "../contracts";
import type { StoredSummary } from "../db/store/summaries";
import { errorResponseFor } from "../engine/errors";
import type { IndexResult } from "../index/pipeline";
import type { ChildMessage, ParentMessage } from "./protocol";
import type { BackgroundService } from "./service";

/** POST /api/summarize's answer, from the row summarizeNow returns. */
export function toSummarizeResponse(row: StoredSummary): SummarizeResponse {
    return {
        path: row.path,
        kind: row.kind,
        ...(row.tagline ? { tagline: row.tagline, summary: row.summary ?? "" } : {}),
        summaryStatus: row.status,
        ...(row.status === "failed" && row.error ? { summaryError: row.error } : {}),
    };
}

/** POST /api/index's `done` event, from a structure run's result. */
export function toIndexDone(result: IndexResult): IndexEventMap["done"] {
    return {
        files: result.files,
        changed: result.changed,
        deleted: result.deleted.length,
        edges: result.edges,
        folders: result.folders,
        levelEdges: result.levelEdges,
        errors: result.errors,
        skipped: result.skipped.length,
        totalMs: Math.round(result.totalMs),
    };
}

/**
 * Does what one message from the server asks, and answers it. A failure is an answer too, with the code the API
 * shows, so the server never waits on a request the worker dropped.
 */
export async function handleParentMessage(service: BackgroundService, message: ParentMessage, send: (reply: ChildMessage) => void): Promise<void> {
    switch (message.type){
        case "repo":
            await service.setRepo(message.root);
            send({ type: "ready" });
            return;

        case "summarize":
            try{
                send({ type: "summarized", id: message.id, outcome: { ok: true, summary: toSummarizeResponse(await service.summarizeNow(message.path)) } });
            } catch (err){
                send({ type: "summarized", id: message.id, outcome: { ok: false, error: errorResponseFor(err).error } });
            }
            return;

        case "index":
            try{
                const result = await service.indexNow((stage, done, total) => send({ type: "index-progress", id: message.id, progress: { stage, done, total } }));
                send({ type: "indexed", id: message.id, outcome: { ok: true, result: toIndexDone(result) } });
            } catch (err){
                send({ type: "indexed", id: message.id, outcome: { ok: false, error: errorResponseFor(err).error } });
            }
            return;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The supervisor** — `lib/background/supervisor.ts`:
```ts
import type { IndexEventMap } from "../contracts";
import type { ApiFailure, ChildMessage, IndexOutcome, ParentMessage, SummarizeOutcome } from "./protocol";

/** The part of a ChildProcess the supervisor uses, so tests can hand it a fake. `send` is false when nothing received it. */
export type ChildLike = {
    send(message: ParentMessage): boolean;
    onMessage(listener: (message: ChildMessage) => void): void;
    onExit(listener: (code: number | null) => void): void;
    kill(): void;
};

export type Supervisor = {
    /** Points the worker at a repo (null: none), now and after every restart; starts the worker on first use. */
    setRepo(root: string | null): void;
    summarize(targetPath: string): Promise<SummarizeOutcome>;
    index(onProgress: (progress: IndexEventMap["progress"]) => void): Promise<IndexOutcome>;
    stop(): void;
};

/** How long to wait before each restart of a worker that died: growing, so a crash loop can't spin. The last one repeats. */
export const RESTART_DELAYS_MS = [1_000, 2_000, 5_000, 10_000, 30_000] as const;

const DOWN: ApiFailure["error"] = { code: "INTERNAL", message: "The background worker isn't running right now (it restarts on its own); try again in a moment." };
const GONE: ApiFailure["error"] = { code: "INTERNAL", message: "The background worker stopped before answering; try again." };

type Pending = {
    settle: (reply: ChildMessage) => void;
    fail: (error: ApiFailure["error"]) => void;
    onProgress?: (progress: IndexEventMap["progress"]) => void;
};

/**
 * The server's side of the background worker (ADR-038). It starts the worker, sends it requests and matches the answers
 * by id. When the worker dies, everything it owed is answered with an error, and a new one starts after a growing
 * delay, pointed at the same repo. While none is running, requests fail at once instead of waiting.
 */
export function createSupervisor(spawn: () => ChildLike, options: { log?: (message: string) => void } = {}): Supervisor {
    const log = options.log ?? ((message: string) => console.log(`[background] ${message}`));
    const pending = new Map<number, Pending>();
    let child: ChildLike | null = null;
    let repo: string | null = null;
    let nextId = 1;
    let crashes = 0;
    let stopped = false;
    let restartTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

    const start = (): void => {
        const current = spawn();
        child = current;

        current.onMessage((message) => {
            if (message.type === "ready"){
                crashes = 0;   // it came up: the next crash starts the delays over
                return;
            }
            if (message.type === "index-progress"){
                pending.get(message.id)?.onProgress?.(message.progress);
                return;
            }
            const waiting = pending.get(message.id);
            if (!waiting) return;
            pending.delete(message.id);
            waiting.settle(message);
        });

        current.onExit((code) => {
            if (child !== current) return;
            child = null;
            // everything this worker owed is answered now, as a failure: nothing waits on a dead process
            for (const waiting of pending.values()) waiting.fail(GONE);
            pending.clear();
            if (stopped) return;

            const delay = RESTART_DELAYS_MS[Math.min(crashes, RESTART_DELAYS_MS.length - 1)];
            crashes++;
            log(`the worker stopped (exit code ${code}); starting a new one in ${delay / 1000} s`);
            restartTimer = setTimeout(() => {
                restartTimer = null;
                if (!stopped) start();
            }, delay);
        });

        current.send({ type: "repo", root: repo });
    };

    /** Registers a request and sends it: resolves with the worker's reply, or with a failure when it can't answer. */
    const ask = (build: (id: number) => ParentMessage, onProgress?: Pending["onProgress"]): Promise<ChildMessage | ApiFailure> => {
        const current = child;
        if (!current) return Promise.resolve({ ok: false, error: DOWN });

        const id = nextId++;
        return new Promise((resolve) => {
            pending.set(id, { settle: resolve, fail: (error) => resolve({ ok: false, error }), onProgress });
            if (!current.send(build(id))){
                pending.delete(id);
                resolve({ ok: false, error: DOWN });
            }
        });
    };

    return {
        setRepo(root){
            repo = root;
            if (stopped) return;
            if (child) child.send({ type: "repo", root });
            else if (restartTimer === null) start();
        },

        async summarize(targetPath){
            const reply = await ask((id) => ({ type: "summarize", id, path: targetPath }));
            if (!("type" in reply)) return reply;
            return reply.type === "summarized" ? reply.outcome : { ok: false, error: GONE };
        },

        async index(onProgress){
            const reply = await ask((id) => ({ type: "index", id }), onProgress);
            if (!("type" in reply)) return reply;
            return reply.type === "indexed" ? reply.outcome : { ok: false, error: GONE };
        },

        stop(){
            stopped = true;
            if (restartTimer) clearTimeout(restartTimer);
            restartTimer = null;
            child?.kill();
        },
    };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: The real process.**
  1. `lib/background/start.ts`:
```ts
import { fork } from "child_process";
import { readConfig } from "../config";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { nebulosityHome, nebulosityPath } from "../home";
import type { ChildMessage } from "./protocol";
import { createSupervisor, type ChildLike, type Supervisor } from "./supervisor";

// on globalThis so a hot reload in development keeps its one worker instead of forking another
const globalForBackground = globalThis as unknown as { nebulosityBackground?: Supervisor };

/**
 * The server's one background worker (ADR-038), started on first use. The database is opened (and migrated) here
 * first, so the worker never races the server through a new migration.
 */
export function backgroundSupervisor(): Supervisor {
    if (!globalForBackground.nebulosityBackground){
        getDb();
        const supervisor = createSupervisor(spawnWorker);
        supervisor.setRepo(readConfig().activeRepo);
        globalForBackground.nebulosityBackground = supervisor;
    }
    return globalForBackground.nebulosityBackground;
}

/** Forks scripts/background.ts through tsx, the way every CLI script runs; `--import tsx` resolves from the checkout (cwd). */
function spawnWorker(): ChildLike {
    const child = fork(nebulosityPath("scripts", "background.ts"), [], {
        cwd: nebulosityHome(),
        execArgv: ["--import", "tsx"],
        stdio: ["ignore", "inherit", "inherit", "ipc"],
    });

    const exitListeners: ((code: number | null) => void)[] = [];
    let exited = false;
    const exit = (code: number | null): void => {
        if (exited) return;
        exited = true;
        for (const listener of exitListeners) listener(code);
    };
    child.on("exit", (code) => exit(code));
    // a worker that can't start at all (tsx missing, a bad path) reports an error and may never exit: treat it as one that did
    child.on("error", (err) => {
        console.error(`[background] the worker failed: ${err.message}`);
        exit(null);
    });

    return {
        send: (message) => child.connected && child.send(message),
        onMessage: (listener) => { child.on("message", (message) => listener(message as ChildMessage)); },
        onExit: (listener) => { exitListeners.push(listener); },
        kill: () => { child.kill(); },
    };
}
```
  2. `scripts/background.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * The background worker (ADR-038). The Map's server forks this file through tsx; don't run it by hand. It owns every
 * write while the Map is open: the file watcher, incremental structure updates, summary refreshes, the folder sweep and
 * embeddings. It answers the server over the IPC channel (lib/background/protocol.ts) and exits when the server goes away.
 */
import { handleParentMessage } from "../lib/background/child";
import type { ChildMessage, ParentMessage } from "../lib/background/protocol";
import { createBackgroundService } from "../lib/background/service";
import { summarizerFromEnv } from "../lib/index/summarize/claude";
import { loadLocalEnv } from "./env";

loadLocalEnv();

const send = process.send?.bind(process);
if (!send){
    console.error("[background] this is the Map server's worker: start it with npm run dev, not by hand");
    process.exit(1);
}

const service = createBackgroundService({
    summarizer: summarizerFromEnv(),
    log: (message) => console.log(`[background] ${message}`),
});

process.on("message", (message: unknown) => {
    handleParentMessage(service, message as ParentMessage, (reply: ChildMessage) => { send(reply); })
        .catch((err: unknown) => console.error("[background]", err));
});

// the server is gone (stopped, crashed, restarted): stop watching and exit, so no orphan keeps writing
process.on("disconnect", () => {
    void service.stop().finally(() => process.exit(0));
});
```
  3. `instrumentation.ts`, at the repo root:
```ts
/**
 * Runs once when the Next server starts (node_modules/next/dist/docs/01-app/02-guides/instrumentation.md). It starts
 * the background worker (ADR-038), which watches the active repo and keeps its index current while the Map is open.
 */
export async function register(): Promise<void> {
    if (process.env.NEXT_RUNTIME !== "nodejs") return;
    // next build loads this file too, and a file watcher has no business running during a build
    if (process.env.NEXT_PHASE === "phase-production-build") return;
    const { backgroundSupervisor } = await import("./lib/background/start");
    backgroundSupervisor();
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The port in the config** — replace `lib/config.ts`'s type and `readConfig`:
```ts
/** The port the Map's server listens on (npm run dev's default); live events are posted to it (PIVOT_SPEC.md §8). */
export const DEFAULT_PORT = 3000;

/** Small user-level settings that outlive any one run. */
export type NebulosityConfig = { activeRepo: string | null; port: number };

export function readConfig(): NebulosityConfig {
    try{
        const raw = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(configFile(), "utf8")) as { activeRepo?: unknown; port?: unknown };
        const port = typeof raw.port === "number" && Number.isInteger(raw.port) && raw.port > 0 && raw.port < 65_536 ? raw.port : DEFAULT_PORT;
        return { activeRepo: typeof raw.activeRepo === "string" ? raw.activeRepo : null, port };
    } catch{
        return { activeRepo: null, port: DEFAULT_PORT };   // missing or unreadable: nothing chosen yet
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The live modules.**
  1. `lib/live/hub.ts`:
```ts
import type { LiveEvent } from "../contracts";

export type LiveListener = (event: LiveEvent) => void;

/** At most this many events a second reach the open Maps, so a runaway agent can't flood a browser tab. */
export const MAX_EVENTS_PER_SECOND = 20;

type Hub = { listeners: Set<LiveListener>; sentAt: number[] };

// on globalThis so a hot reload in development keeps the Maps already listening
const globalForHub = globalThis as unknown as { nebulosityLiveHub?: Hub };
const hub: Hub = (globalForHub.nebulosityLiveHub ??= { listeners: new Set(), sentAt: [] });

/** Starts handing live events to `listener` (one per open Map); returns the function that stops it. */
export function subscribe(listener: LiveListener): () => void {
    hub.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => { hub.listeners.delete(listener); };
}

/**
 * Hands an event to every open Map (PIVOT_SPEC.md §8). False when it was dropped for the rate limit. A listener
 * that throws (its stream has closed) is dropped, and the rest still get the event.
 */
export function publish(event: LiveEvent, now: number = Date.now()): boolean {
    hub.sentAt = hub.sentAt.filter((t) => now - t < 1000);
    if (hub.sentAt.length >= MAX_EVENTS_PER_SECOND) return false;
    hub.sentAt.push(now);

    for (const listener of [...hub.listeners]){
        try{
            listener(event);
        } catch{
            hub.listeners.delete(listener);
        }
    }
    return true;
}
```
  2. `lib/live/event.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import type { LiveEvent, UIAction } from "../contracts";
import { parseRepoPath } from "../index/paths";

const actionSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
    z.object({ type: z.literal("highlight_nodes"), nodeIds: z.array(z.string().max(1024)).max(50) }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("focus_node"), nodeId: z.string().max(1024) }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("clear_highlights") }),
]);

const eventSchema = z.object({
    tool: z.string().trim().min(1).max(64),
    paths: z.array(z.string().max(1024)).max(50).default([]),
    at: z.string().max(64).optional(),
    action: actionSchema.optional(),
});

const repoPaths = (raw: readonly string[]): string[] => raw.map(parseRepoPath).filter((p): p is string => p !== null);

/**
 * A live event from outside (an MCP tool handler, npm run live), checked and normalized: repo-relative paths only (any
 * other is dropped), `at` filled in when missing. Null when the body isn't a live event, or names a focus target
 * outside the repo.
 */
export function parseLiveEvent(raw: unknown, now: Date = new Date()): LiveEvent | null {
    const parsed = eventSchema.safeParse(raw);
    if (!parsed.success) return null;

    let action: UIAction | undefined;
    const given = parsed.data.action;
    if (given?.type === "highlight_nodes") action = { type: "highlight_nodes", nodeIds: repoPaths(given.nodeIds) };
    else if (given?.type === "clear_highlights") action = { type: "clear_highlights" };
    else if (given?.type === "focus_node"){
        const nodeId = parseRepoPath(given.nodeId);
        if (nodeId === null) return null;
        action = { type: "focus_node", nodeId };
    }

    return { tool: parsed.data.tool, paths: repoPaths(parsed.data.paths), at: parsed.data.at ?? now.toISOString(), ...(action ? { action } : {}) };
}
```
  3. `lib/live/sse.ts`:
```ts
/** One Server-Sent Events frame (API_CONTRACTS.md §1): the event's name, its data as JSON, and a blank line. */
export function sseFrame(event: string, data: unknown): string {
    return `event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`;
}

/** A comment line: browsers ignore it, and it keeps an idle stream's socket from being closed. */
export const SSE_KEEP_ALIVE = ": keep-alive\n\n";

export const SSE_HEADERS = {
    "Content-Type": "text/event-stream",
    "Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-transform",
    Connection: "keep-alive",
} as const;
```
  4. `lib/live/local.ts`:
```ts
const LOCAL_HOSTNAMES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "[::1]"]);

/** The host name in a Host header or an origin: "localhost:3000" → "localhost", "[::1]:3000" → "[::1]". */
function hostnameOf(hostAndPort: string): string | null {
    try{
        return new URL(`http://${hostAndPort}`).hostname;
    } catch{
        return null;
    }
}

/**
 * Whether a request comes from this machine and from no other web page (ADR-039), the guard on every route that
 * spends money, changes the index or drives the Map.
 * - **Host.** It must name this machine. A page on another site that reaches 127.0.0.1 through DNS rebinding still
 *   sends its own host name.
 * - **Origin.** A browser request from any page carries that page's Origin, which must be this machine as well.
 *   Programs (the MCP server's fetch, curl) send none.
 */
export function isLocalRequest(headers: { get(name: string): string | null }): boolean {
    const host = headers.get("host");
    const hostname = host === null ? null : hostnameOf(host);
    if (hostname === null || !LOCAL_HOSTNAMES.has(hostname)) return false;

    const origin = headers.get("origin");
    if (origin === null) return true;
    try{
        return LOCAL_HOSTNAMES.has(new URL(origin).hostname);
    } catch{
        return false;   // "null": a sandboxed frame or a file:// page
    }
}
```
  5. `lib/live/client.ts`:
```ts
import type { LiveEvent } from "../contracts";
import { readConfig } from "../config";

/**
 * Tells the open Maps about a tool call (PIVOT_SPEC.md §8): one POST to the local server, with a 200 ms timeout.
 * Every failure is swallowed, because no Map being open is the normal case. True when the server took the event.
 */
export async function postLiveEvent(
    event: LiveEvent,
    options: { port?: number; timeoutMs?: number; fetchFn?: typeof fetch } = {}
): Promise<boolean> {
    const port = options.port ?? readConfig().port;
    try{
        const response = await (options.fetchFn ?? fetch)(`http://127.0.0.1:${port}/api/live`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
            body: JSON.stringify(event),
            signal: AbortSignal.timeout(options.timeoutMs ?? 200),
        });
        return response.ok;
    } catch{
        return false;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: The routes.**
  1. `app/api/live/route.ts`:
```ts
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import { parseLiveEvent } from "@/lib/live/event";
import { publish } from "@/lib/live/hub";
import { isLocalRequest } from "@/lib/live/local";
import { forbidden, jsonError } from "../_lib/respond";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

// POST /api/live — a tool call to show on the open Maps; this machine only (API_CONTRACTS.md §14, ADR-039)
export async function POST(request: NextRequest){
    if (!isLocalRequest(request.headers)) return forbidden();
    const event = parseLiveEvent(await request.json().catch(() => null));
    if (event === null) return jsonError(400, "BAD_REQUEST", "Expected { tool, paths?, at?, action? }.");

    publish(event);
    return new Response(null, { status: 204 });
}
```
  2. `app/api/live/stream/route.ts`:
```ts
import { subscribe } from "@/lib/live/hub";
import { SSE_HEADERS, SSE_KEEP_ALIVE, sseFrame } from "@/lib/live/sse";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

const KEEP_ALIVE_MS = 15_000;

// GET /api/live/stream — every live event, as Server-Sent Events, for as long as the Map stays open (API_CONTRACTS.md §14)
export function GET(request: Request){
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();
    let stop = (): void => {};

    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
        start(controller){
            const write = (text: string): void => controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(text));
            write(sseFrame("ready", {}));

            const unsubscribe = subscribe((event) => write(sseFrame("tool", event)));
            const keepAlive = setInterval(() => {
                try{
                    write(SSE_KEEP_ALIVE);
                } catch{
                    stop();   // the stream closed without an abort reaching us
                }
            }, KEEP_ALIVE_MS);
            stop = () => {
                unsubscribe();
                clearInterval(keepAlive);
            };

            request.signal.addEventListener("abort", () => {
                stop();
                try{
                    controller.close();
                } catch{
                    // already closed
                }
            });
        },
        cancel(){
            stop();
        },
    });

    return new Response(stream, { headers: SSE_HEADERS });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: A poster by hand** — `scripts/live.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * npm run live -- <tool> [path ...] [--highlight | --focus | --clear]
 *
 * Sends one live event to the open Map, as V1.4's MCP tools will (PIVOT_SPEC.md §8). The boxes holding the paths
 * pulse. --highlight, --focus or --clear also sends that UI action, which is what show_on_map sends. For trying the
 * Map by hand.
 */
import type { LiveEvent, UIAction } from "../lib/contracts";
import { postLiveEvent } from "../lib/live/client";

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const flags = args.filter((a) => a.startsWith("--"));
const [tool, ...paths] = args.filter((a) => !a.startsWith("--"));
const ACTIONS = ["--highlight", "--focus", "--clear"];

if (!tool || flags.length > 1 || flags.some((f) => !ACTIONS.includes(f)) || (flags[0] === "--focus" && paths.length !== 1)){
    console.error("usage: npm run live -- <tool> [path ...] [--highlight | --focus | --clear]");
    process.exit(1);
}

const action: UIAction | undefined =
    flags[0] === "--highlight" ? { type: "highlight_nodes", nodeIds: paths }
    : flags[0] === "--focus" ? { type: "focus_node", nodeId: paths[0] }
    : flags[0] === "--clear" ? { type: "clear_highlights" }
    : undefined;
const event: LiveEvent = { tool, paths, at: new Date().toISOString(), ...(action ? { action } : {}) };

postLiveEvent(event, { timeoutMs: 2_000 }).then((ok) => {
    console.log(ok ? `[live] sent ${tool} (${paths.length} paths)` : "[live] no Map server answered on the configured port; is npm run dev running?");
    process.exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
});
```

  1. Append to `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md`:
```md

## 14. The live channel (slice V1.3)

`POST /api/live` takes a tool call, and `GET /api/live/stream` sends each one to the open Maps (`PIVOT_SPEC.md` §8, ADR-039).

```ts
type LiveEvent = { tool: string; paths: string[]; at: string; action?: UIAction };
```

- **`POST /api/live`** takes a `LiveEvent` as its body and answers `204`.
  - `at` and `paths` are optional. `paths` holds at most 50 repo-relative paths; others are dropped. `action` is a `UIAction`, whose node ids may be folders.
  - **Localhost only.** The `Host` header must be `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` or `[::1]` (any port), and an `Origin`, if present, one of those too. Anything else gets `403 FORBIDDEN`.
  - A body that isn't a live event gets `400 BAD_REQUEST`.
  - At most 20 events a second are passed on; the rest are dropped.
- **`GET /api/live/stream`** is Server-Sent Events: `ready` (`{}`) once, then `tool` (a `LiveEvent`) per call, and a `: keep-alive` comment every 15 s. It's a GET, so the browser's `EventSource` reads it directly.
- **`postLiveEvent`** (`lib/live/client.ts`) is the poster. It sends one POST to `http://127.0.0.1:<port>/api/live` with a 200 ms timeout and ignores every failure, since no Map being open is the normal case. `port` comes from `.nebulosity/config.json` (default 3000). V1.4's MCP tools call it, and `npm run live -- <tool> [path …] [--highlight | --focus | --clear]` sends one by hand.
```
  2. Append **ADR-039** to `docs/ENGINEERING_DECISIONS.md`:
```md

## ADR-039 — The live channel: SSE out, localhost-only POST in, the server on loopback (‹today›)

**Context:** PIVOT_SPEC.md §8 has MCP tool handlers notify the open Map, with `show_on_map` reusing ADR-008's UI actions. The routes that spend money (summarize), change the index (re-index, switch repo) or drive the Map (live events) are reachable from any web page the user has open: a page can POST to `localhost:3000`, and DNS rebinding can make a foreign host name resolve to 127.0.0.1. `next dev` listens on every interface, so the GET routes would serve code snippets and summaries to the local network.
**Decision:**
- **The event.** A tool call is `{ tool, paths, at, action? }`. The Map pulses the box holding each path at its current level and runs `action` if there is one, so `show_on_map` is just a tool event with an action.
- **The stream.** `GET /api/live/stream` sends events as SSE (ADR-002), with a keep-alive comment every 15 s. The hub that fans events out lives in the server process (`lib/live/hub.ts`), kept on `globalThis` across hot reloads, and passes on at most 20 events a second.
- **The guard.** Every state-changing route checks `isLocalRequest`: the Host header must name this machine, and an Origin, if present, too. Programs send no Origin; a foreign page always does.
- **Loopback.** `npm run dev` and `npm run start` bind to 127.0.0.1.
- **The poster.** It fires and forgets: a 200 ms timeout, failures ignored, the port from the config.
**Alternatives:** (a) A shared secret in a header: the MCP server would need to read it from disk, and it guards nothing a loopback bind and the Host/Origin check don't. (b) WebSockets: two-way, which nothing here needs (ADR-002's reasoning holds).
**Consequences:** No other machine can reach the Map, and no web page can make it spend money or move. Opening the Map from another device (a phone on the same network) would need a deliberate `-H 0.0.0.0` and a token, which is out of scope.
**Revisit when:** the desktop app hosts the Map: the server then lives inside it and the guard moves to its IPC.
```

  2. Append to `lib/db/store/stages.ts`. Add `repositories` to the `../schema` import, and add `sql` to the `drizzle-orm` import.
```ts

/**
 * Repositories the structure stage has indexed: they have a stage row (`stage:<id>:structure`). Rows the retired
 * Phase D pipeline left have none, so they're left out.
 */
export function listStagedRepositories(db: Db): { rootPath: string; lastIndexAt: Date | null }[] {
    return db.select({ rootPath: repositories.rootPath, lastIndexAt: repositories.lastIndexAt })
        .from(repositories)
        .where(sql`exists (select 1 from ${meta} where ${meta.key} = 'stage:' || ${repositories.id} || ':structure')`)
        .orderBy(repositories.rootPath)
        .all();
}
```
  3. `lib/engine/repos.ts`:
```ts
import path from "path";
import type { RepoList } from "../contracts";
import { readConfig, writeConfig } from "../config";
import { getDb } from "../db/client";
import { listStagedRepositories } from "../db/store/stages";

/** Thrown when the picker names a repo that was never indexed. */
export class UnknownRepoError extends Error {
    constructor(root: string){
        super(`${root} isn't an indexed repository. Run npm run index -- "${root}" first.`);
        this.name = "UnknownRepoError";
    }
}

/** The repos the Map can show, and which one is active (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7.1: a picker when more than one is indexed). */
export function listRepos(): RepoList {
    const repos = listStagedRepositories(getDb()).map((r) => ({
        root: r.rootPath,
        name: path.basename(r.rootPath),
        indexedAt: r.lastIndexAt?.toISOString() ?? null,
    }));
    return { active: readConfig().activeRepo, repos };
}

/** Makes an indexed repo the active one (`activeRepo` in .nebulosity/config.json) and returns the new list. */
export function setActiveRepo(root: string): RepoList {
    if (!listRepos().repos.some((r) => r.root === root)) throw new UnknownRepoError(root);
    writeConfig({ ...readConfig(), activeRepo: root });
    return listRepos();
}
```

```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { ErrorEnvelope, SummarizeResponse } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { backgroundSupervisor } from "@/lib/background/start";
import { STATUS_FOR } from "@/lib/engine/errors";
import { parseRepoPath } from "@/lib/index/paths";
import { isLocalRequest } from "@/lib/live/local";
import { activeRepo, badPath, forbidden, noActiveRepo } from "../_lib/respond";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

// POST /api/summarize?path= — summarize one file or folder now, jumping the queue (API_CONTRACTS.md §13).
// It spends money, so only this machine may ask.
export async function POST(request: NextRequest){
    if (!isLocalRequest(request.headers)) return forbidden();
    if (activeRepo() === null) return noActiveRepo();
    const target = parseRepoPath(request.nextUrl.searchParams.get("path"));
    if (target === null) return badPath();

    const outcome = await backgroundSupervisor().summarize(target);
    if (!outcome.ok) return NextResponse.json<ErrorEnvelope>({ error: outcome.error }, { status: STATUS_FOR[outcome.error.code] });
    return NextResponse.json<SummarizeResponse>(outcome.summary);
}
```
  2. Replace `app/api/index/route.ts` with:
```ts
import { NextRequest } from "next/server";
import type { IndexEventMap } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { backgroundSupervisor } from "@/lib/background/start";
import { isLocalRequest } from "@/lib/live/local";
import { SSE_HEADERS, sseFrame } from "@/lib/live/sse";
import { activeRepo, forbidden, noActiveRepo } from "../_lib/respond";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

// POST /api/index — a full structure run of the active repo in the background worker, its progress as SSE (API_CONTRACTS.md §4)
export function POST(request: NextRequest){
    if (!isLocalRequest(request.headers)) return forbidden();
    if (activeRepo() === null) return noActiveRepo();
    const encoder = new TextEncoder();

    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
        async start(controller){
            const send = <K extends keyof IndexEventMap>(event: K, data: IndexEventMap[K]): void => {
                try{
                    controller.enqueue(encoder.encode(sseFrame(event, data)));
                } catch{
                    // the client left; the run goes on in the worker either way
                }
            };
            const outcome = await backgroundSupervisor().index((progress) => send("progress", progress));
            if (outcome.ok) send("done", outcome.result);
            else send("error", outcome.error);
            try{
                controller.close();
            } catch{
                // already closed
            }
        },
    });

    return new Response(stream, { headers: SSE_HEADERS });
}
```
  3. `app/api/repos/route.ts`:
```ts
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from "next/server";
import type { RepoList } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { backgroundSupervisor } from "@/lib/background/start";
import { listRepos, setActiveRepo } from "@/lib/engine/repos";
import { isLocalRequest } from "@/lib/live/local";
import { errorJson, forbidden, jsonError } from "../_lib/respond";

export const runtime = "nodejs";

// GET /api/repos — the indexed repos and the active one; POST { root } switches (API_CONTRACTS.md §15)
export function GET(){
    return NextResponse.json<RepoList>(listRepos());
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest){
    if (!isLocalRequest(request.headers)) return forbidden();
    const body = (await request.json().catch(() => null)) as { root?: unknown } | null;
    if (typeof body?.root !== "string") return jsonError(400, "BAD_REQUEST", "Expected { root }: an indexed repository's root.");

    try{
        const list = setActiveRepo(body.root);
        backgroundSupervisor().setRepo(body.root);   // the worker watches the new repo from now on
        return NextResponse.json<RepoList>(list);
    } catch (err){
        return errorJson(err);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Document it** — in `docs/API_CONTRACTS.md`:
  1. Replace §4 ("POST /api/index — SSE") with:
```md
## 4. POST /api/index — SSE (rewritten in slice V1.3)

A full structure run of the active repo, in the background worker (ADR-038), with its progress as SSE. Localhost only (§14's guard: `403 FORBIDDEN` otherwise); `404 INDEX_EMPTY` without an active repo. Body: none.

| event | data | notes |
|---|---|---|
| `progress` | `{ stage: "discover" \| "parse" \| "write" \| "resolve" \| "aggregate", done, total }` | at least once per stage, in order |
| `done` | `{ files, changed, deleted, edges, folders, levelEdges, errors[], skipped, totalMs }` | terminal on success; `errors` are per-file failures (ADR-021) |
| `error` | `{ code, message }` | terminal on failure: `INDEX_LOCKED` while another run holds the lock, `INTERNAL` if the worker stopped |

The run goes on in the worker if the client leaves.
```
  2. Append:
```md

## 13. POST /api/summarize?path= (slice V1.3)

Summarizes one file or folder now through the regular API, jumping the queue (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.3). The Map sends it when it opens a target whose summary is `pending` or `stale`; it never sends it when summaries are `off`. Localhost only, because it spends money.

```ts
type SummarizeResponse = { path: string; kind: "repo" | "folder" | "file"; tagline?: string; summary?: string;
  summaryStatus: SummaryStatus; summaryError?: string };
```

- **Current summaries.** A current summary comes back as it is, with no API call.
- **Duplicates.** Concurrent requests for one target share one request (ADR-033, amended).
- **Status codes.**
  - `409 NOT_READY`: a folder whose files or subfolders aren't all summarized yet. Its summary would be written over "(no summary yet)".
  - `409 SUMMARIES_OFF`: no API key.
  - `404 NOT_FOUND`, `400 BAD_REQUEST` and `403 FORBIDDEN` as usual.
  - `500 INTERNAL` when the background worker stopped before answering.
- **Afterwards.** The summary lands in the index, so the index version moves and the Map refetches. Its vector follows within seconds.

## 15. GET /api/repos, POST /api/repos (slice V1.3)

The repo picker (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7.1).

```ts
type RepoSummary = { root: string; name: string; indexedAt: string | null };
type RepoList = { active: string | null; repos: RepoSummary[] };
```

- **`GET`** lists the repos the structure stage has indexed, by root, and the active one.
- **`POST { root }`** makes an indexed repo the active one (`activeRepo` in `.nebulosity/config.json`) and points the background worker at it. It answers with the new `RepoList`. Localhost only. A root that was never indexed gets `404 NOT_FOUND`.
```

### Task 16: The Map's client foundations

Everything the UI decides that can be decided without a browser, in pure modules with tests, plus the one module that talks to the server.

**Lint rules for every UI task.** The repo's lint runs React 19's hook rules as errors (`eslint-plugin-react-hooks` 7: `set-state-in-effect`, `refs`, `purity`, `immutability`, `static-components`, `preserve-manual-memoization`). The UI code in Tasks 16–20 is written to pass them:
- **No synchronous setState in an effect body.** State is set in promise callbacks, timers and event handlers.
- **No ref read during render.**
- **No `Date.now()` in render.**
- **No component defined inside another.**
- **New Maps and Sets instead of mutated ones.**

React Flow's `setNodes`/`setEdges` come from its own hook and pass, as the old `GraphCanvas` showed.

**Files:**
- Create:
  - `app/map/api.ts`, `paths.ts`, `layout.ts`, `edges.ts`, `format.ts`, `summaries.ts`, `pulses.ts`, `perf.ts`
  - their tests: `app/map/api.test.ts`, `paths.test.ts`, `layout.test.ts`, `edges.test.ts`, `format.test.ts`, `summaries.test.ts`, `pulses.test.ts`
- Modify: `package.json`, `package-lock.json` (`@dagrejs/dagre`)

**Interfaces** (every later UI task uses these names):
- `api.ts`:
  - `class ApiError(status, code: ErrorCode | "NETWORK", message)`;
  - `levelUrl(path)`, `describeUrl(path)`;
  - `api.{ level, describe, search, status, summarize, repos, setActiveRepo, reindex }`;
  - `type SseFrame`, `parseSseFrames(buffer)`, `readSseStream(body, onFrame)`.
- `paths.ts`: `parentOf`, `nameOf(path, repoRoot)`, `isInside`, `boxFor(level, path)`.
- `layout.ts`: `BOX_WIDTH = 224`, `BOX_HEIGHT = 76`, `type Position`, `layoutLevel(nodes, edges): Map<string, Position>`.
- `edges.ts`: `edgeWidth(count, maxCount)`, `edgeOpacity(edge, selected)`.
- `format.ts`: `formatCount`, `ago(ms)`, `stageLabel(name, stage)`, `type Tone`, `headline(status)`.
- `summaries.ts`: `wantsSummary(status)`, `placeholderFor(status)`.
- `pulses.ts`: `PULSE_GAP_MS = 800`, `type Pulse`, `nextPulses(state, boxes, now)`.
- `perf.ts`: `markStart(name)`, `measureAfterPaint(name)`.

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { api, ApiError, levelUrl, parseSseFrames } from "./api";

afterEach(() => { vi.unstubAllGlobals(); });

describe("parseSseFrames", () => {
    it("splits whole frames, joins multi-line data, skips comments, and keeps a cut-off frame for the next chunk", () => {
        const { frames, rest } = parseSseFrames('event: tool\ndata: {"a":1}\n\n: keep-alive\n\nevent: x\ndata: one\ndata: two\n\nevent: half\ndata: {"b"');
        expect(frames).toEqual([{ event: "tool", data: '{"a":1}' }, { event: "x", data: "one\ntwo" }]);
        expect(rest).toBe('event: half\ndata: {"b"');
    });
});

describe("api", () => {
    it("percent-encodes paths, so spaces, #, %, + and non-ASCII letters reach the server intact", () => {
        expect(levelUrl("src/my file#1%+é.ts")).toBe("/api/level?path=src%2Fmy%20file%231%25%2B%C3%A9.ts");
    });

    it("turns an error envelope into an ApiError with the server's code, and a dead server into NETWORK", async () => {
        vi.stubGlobal("fetch", async () => new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: { code: "NOT_FOUND", message: "Nothing is indexed at \"x\"." } }), { status: 404 }));
        await expect(api.describe("x")).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 404, code: "NOT_FOUND", message: "Nothing is indexed at \"x\"." });

        vi.stubGlobal("fetch", async () => { throw new TypeError("Failed to fetch"); });
        await expect(api.level("")).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
        await expect(api.level("")).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "NETWORK" });
    });
});

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { MapEdge, MapNode } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { BOX_HEIGHT, BOX_WIDTH, layoutLevel } from "./layout";

const node = (p: string): MapNode => ({ path: p, name: p, kind: "folder", fileCount: 1, summaryStatus: "fresh" });

describe("layoutLevel", () => {
    it("puts importers above what they import", () => {
        const at = layoutLevel([node("a"), node("b"), node("c")], [{ source: "a", target: "b", count: 3 }, { source: "b", target: "c", count: 1 }]);
        expect(at.get("a")!.y).toBeLessThan(at.get("b")!.y);
        expect(at.get("b")!.y).toBeLessThan(at.get("c")!.y);
    });

    it("lays boxes with no lines in a grid below the rest, in the level's order", () => {
        const at = layoutLevel([node("a"), node("b"), ...["l1", "l2", "l3", "l4"].map(node)], [{ source: "a", target: "b", count: 1 }]);
        const bottomOfTheRest = Math.max(at.get("a")!.y, at.get("b")!.y) + BOX_HEIGHT;
        // four loose boxes make a 2 × 2 grid, 28 px apart: l1 l2 / l3 l4
        expect(at.get("l1")!.x).toBe(0);
        expect(at.get("l1")!.y).toBeGreaterThan(bottomOfTheRest);
        expect(at.get("l2")).toEqual({ x: BOX_WIDTH + 28, y: at.get("l1")!.y });
        expect(at.get("l3")).toEqual({ x: 0, y: at.get("l1")!.y + BOX_HEIGHT + 28 });
    });

    it("draws the same level the same way every time", () => {
        const nodes = ["a", "b", "c", "d"].map(node);
        const edges: MapEdge[] = [{ source: "a", target: "c", count: 2 }, { source: "b", target: "c", count: 1 }, { source: "d", target: "a", count: 4 }];
        expect([...layoutLevel(nodes, edges)]).toEqual([...layoutLevel(nodes, edges)]);
    });
});
