  1. `app/map/api.ts`:
```ts
import type {
    DescribeResponse, ErrorCode, ErrorEnvelope, IndexEventMap, IndexStatus, LevelResponse, RepoList, SearchResult, SummarizeResponse,
} from "@/lib/contracts";

/**
 * The Map's one door to the server (ARCHITECTURE.md §3, rule 3). Every request goes through here, so a desktop build,
 * or V1.5's static demo, swaps the transport in this one file.
 */

/** A request that failed: the server's error code, or NETWORK when no answer came back at all. */
export class ApiError extends Error {
    constructor(readonly status: number, readonly code: ErrorCode | "NETWORK", message: string){
        super(message);
        this.name = "ApiError";
    }
}

async function failure(response: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
    const body = (await response.json().catch(() => null)) as ErrorEnvelope | null;
    return new ApiError(response.status, body?.error.code ?? "INTERNAL", body?.error.message ?? `HTTP ${response.status}`);
}

async function send(url: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
    try{
        return await fetch(url, init);
    } catch (err){
        if (err instanceof DOMException && err.name === "AbortError") throw err;   // the caller cancelled: not a failure
        throw new ApiError(0, "NETWORK", "The Nebulosity server didn't answer. Is npm run dev still running?");
    }
}

async function request<T>(url: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
    const response = await send(url, init);
    if (!response.ok) throw await failure(response);
    return (await response.json()) as T;
}

/** Paths go in query strings percent-encoded, so spaces, #, %, + and non-ASCII names survive the trip. */
export const levelUrl = (folderPath: string): string => `/api/level?path=${encodeURIComponent(folderPath)}`;
export const describeUrl = (targetPath: string): string => `/api/describe?path=${encodeURIComponent(targetPath)}`;

export type SseFrame = { event: string; data: string };

/**
 * Splits Server-Sent Events text into whole frames (API_CONTRACTS.md §1). A frame cut off at the end of a chunk comes
 * back as `rest`, to go in front of the next chunk. Comment lines (": keep-alive") are skipped.
 */
export function parseSseFrames(buffer: string): { frames: SseFrame[]; rest: string } {
    const parts = buffer.replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").split("\n\n");
    const rest = parts.pop() ?? "";
    const frames: SseFrame[] = [];

    for (const part of parts){
        let event = "message";
        const data: string[] = [];
        for (const line of part.split("\n")){
            if (line.startsWith(":")) continue;
            if (line.startsWith("event:")) event = line.slice(6).trim();
            else if (line.startsWith("data:")) data.push(line.slice(5).replace(/^ /, ""));
        }
        if (data.length > 0) frames.push({ event, data: data.join("\n") });
    }

    return { frames, rest };
}

/** Reads an SSE response body to its end, handing each whole frame to `onFrame`. A POST stream can't use EventSource. */
export async function readSseStream(body: ReadableStream<Uint8Array>, onFrame: (frame: SseFrame) => void): Promise<void> {
    const reader = body.getReader();
    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
    let buffer = "";
    for (;;){
        const { value, done } = await reader.read();
        if (done) return;
        buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
        const parsed = parseSseFrames(buffer);
        buffer = parsed.rest;
        for (const frame of parsed.frames) onFrame(frame);
    }
}

export const api = {
    level: (folderPath: string, signal?: AbortSignal) => request<LevelResponse>(levelUrl(folderPath), { signal }),
    describe: (targetPath: string, signal?: AbortSignal) => request<DescribeResponse>(describeUrl(targetPath), { signal }),
    search: (query: string, limit: number, signal?: AbortSignal) =>
        request<SearchResult>(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(query)}&limit=${limit}`, { signal }),
    status: (signal?: AbortSignal) => request<IndexStatus>("/api/status", { signal }),
    summarize: (targetPath: string) => request<SummarizeResponse>(`/api/summarize?path=${encodeURIComponent(targetPath)}`, { method: "POST" }),
    repos: () => request<RepoList>("/api/repos"),
    setActiveRepo: (root: string) => request<RepoList>("/api/repos", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ root }),
    }),

    /** POST /api/index: a full structure run. Its progress goes to `onProgress`, and the `done` event is the result. */
    async reindex(onProgress: (progress: IndexEventMap["progress"]) => void): Promise<IndexEventMap["done"]> {
        const response = await send("/api/index", { method: "POST" });
        if (!response.ok || !response.body) throw await failure(response);

        const outcome: { done: IndexEventMap["done"] | null; error: IndexEventMap["error"] | null } = { done: null, error: null };
        await readSseStream(response.body, (frame) => {
            if (frame.event === "progress") onProgress(JSON.parse(frame.data) as IndexEventMap["progress"]);
            else if (frame.event === "done") outcome.done = JSON.parse(frame.data) as IndexEventMap["done"];
            else if (frame.event === "error") outcome.error = JSON.parse(frame.data) as IndexEventMap["error"];
        });
        if (outcome.error) throw new ApiError(500, outcome.error.code, outcome.error.message);
        if (!outcome.done) throw new ApiError(500, "INTERNAL", "The re-index ended without a result.");
        return outcome.done;
    },
};
```
  2. `app/map/paths.ts`:
```ts
import type { LevelResponse } from "@/lib/contracts";

// The UI imports nothing from lib/ but types (ARCHITECTURE.md §3), so these few lines mirror lib/index/paths.ts.

/** The folder that holds a path ("" for something at the root). */
export function parentOf(targetPath: string): string {
    const slash = targetPath.lastIndexOf("/");
    return slash === -1 ? "" : targetPath.slice(0, slash);
}

/** What a path is called on screen: its last segment, or the repo's folder name for the root. */
export function nameOf(targetPath: string, repoRoot: string): string {
    if (targetPath === "") return repoRoot.slice(repoRoot.lastIndexOf("/") + 1) || repoRoot;
    return targetPath.slice(targetPath.lastIndexOf("/") + 1);
}

/** Whether `targetPath` is strictly inside `folder` ("" holds everything but itself). */
export function isInside(targetPath: string, folder: string): boolean {
    return folder === "" ? targetPath !== "" : targetPath.startsWith(`${folder}/`);
}

/**
 * The box on this level that holds `targetPath`: the child on the way down to it, or "+N more" when that child is
 * folded. Null when the path isn't inside this level, or is the level itself. Live pulses and highlights use it.
 */
export function boxFor(level: Pick<LevelResponse, "path" | "nodes">, targetPath: string): string | null {
    if (!isInside(targetPath, level.path)) return null;
    const below = level.path === "" ? targetPath : targetPath.slice(level.path.length + 1);
    const slash = below.indexOf("/");
    const child = slash === -1 ? targetPath : `${level.path === "" ? "" : `${level.path}/`}${below.slice(0, slash)}`;
    if (level.nodes.some((n) => n.path === child)) return child;
    return level.nodes.find((n) => n.kind === "more")?.path ?? null;
}
```

  3. `app/map/layout.ts`:
```ts
import dagre from "@dagrejs/dagre";
import type { MapEdge, MapNode } from "@/lib/contracts";

/** Every box is drawn the same size, so the layout needs no measuring. */
export const BOX_WIDTH = 224;
export const BOX_HEIGHT = 76;
const GAP = 28;

export type Position = { x: number; y: number };

/**
 * Where each box of a level goes (ADR-011, PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): dagre's layered layout, importers above what they
 * import. A box with no line to any other at this level adds nothing to the layering, so those go in a grid below
 * the rest, in the level's order. Pure and deterministic: the same level always draws the same picture. React Flow
 * places a node by its top-left corner and dagre by its centre, hence the half-box shift.
 */
export function layoutLevel(nodes: readonly MapNode[], edges: readonly MapEdge[]): Map<string, Position> {
    const positions = new Map<string, Position>();
    const lines = edges.filter((e) => e.source !== e.target);
    const linked = new Set(lines.flatMap((e) => [e.source, e.target]));
    const connected = nodes.filter((n) => linked.has(n.path));
    const loose = nodes.filter((n) => !linked.has(n.path));
    let gridTop = 0;

    if (connected.length > 0){
        const graph = new dagre.graphlib.Graph();
        graph.setGraph({ rankdir: "TB", nodesep: GAP, ranksep: 72, marginx: 0, marginy: 0 });
        graph.setDefaultEdgeLabel(() => ({}));
        for (const node of connected) graph.setNode(node.path, { width: BOX_WIDTH, height: BOX_HEIGHT });
        // a heavier line pulls its boxes closer; the square root keeps 20,000 imports from flattening everything else
        for (const edge of lines) graph.setEdge(edge.source, edge.target, { weight: Math.max(1, Math.round(Math.sqrt(edge.count))) });
        dagre.layout(graph);

        for (const node of connected){
            const centre = graph.node(node.path) as { x: number; y: number };
            positions.set(node.path, { x: centre.x - BOX_WIDTH / 2, y: centre.y - BOX_HEIGHT / 2 });
            gridTop = Math.max(gridTop, centre.y + BOX_HEIGHT / 2 + GAP * 2);
        }
    }

    const columns = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(loose.length)));
    loose.forEach((node, i) => {
        positions.set(node.path, { x: (i % columns) * (BOX_WIDTH + GAP), y: gridTop + Math.floor(i / columns) * (BOX_HEIGHT + GAP) });
    });

    return positions;
}
```
  4. `app/map/edges.ts`:
```ts
import type { MapEdge } from "@/lib/contracts";

export const EDGE_MIN_WIDTH = 1;
export const EDGE_MAX_WIDTH = 6;

/** A line's thickness ∝ √count (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7), scaled so the level's heaviest line is EDGE_MAX_WIDTH wide. */
export function edgeWidth(count: number, maxCount: number): number {
    if (maxCount <= 0) return EDGE_MIN_WIDTH;
    return Math.max(EDGE_MIN_WIDTH, EDGE_MAX_WIDTH * Math.sqrt(count / maxCount));
}

/**
 * How strongly a line is drawn (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): the selected box's lines at full strength, the rest ghosted. With
 * nothing selected every line is faint, so the level's shape still shows.
 */
export function edgeOpacity(edge: MapEdge, selected: string | null): number {
    if (selected === null) return 0.3;
    return edge.source === selected || edge.target === selected ? 0.95 : 0.06;
}
```
  5. `app/map/format.ts`:
```ts
import type { IndexStatus, StageName, StageStatus } from "@/lib/contracts";

/** 20648 → "20,648". */
export function formatCount(n: number): string {
    return n.toLocaleString("en-US");
}

/** How long ago something happened, in words: "just now", "12 s ago", "3 min ago", "2 h ago". */
export function ago(ms: number): string {
    if (ms < 5_000) return "just now";
    if (ms < 60_000) return `${Math.floor(ms / 1000)} s ago`;
    if (ms < 3_600_000) return `${Math.floor(ms / 60_000)} min ago`;
    return `${Math.floor(ms / 3_600_000)} h ago`;
}

const TITLES: Record<StageName, string> = { structure: "Structure", summaries: "Summaries", embeddings: "Search vectors" };
const VERBS: Record<StageName, string> = { structure: "Indexing", summaries: "Summarizing", embeddings: "Embedding" };
const ORDER: readonly StageName[] = ["structure", "summaries", "embeddings"];

const progress = (stage: StageStatus): string => (stage.total > 0 ? ` ${formatCount(stage.done)} / ${formatCount(stage.total)}` : "");

/** One stage as the status menu lists it: "Summaries 12,340 / 18,637 · ready". */
export function stageLabel(name: StageName, stage: StageStatus): string {
    return `${TITLES[name]}${progress(stage)} · ${stage.state}`;
}

export type Tone = "ok" | "busy" | "warn" | "off";

/**
 * The status pill's one line: the stage that most needs attention. A running stage comes first, then a failed one.
 * Then structure not ready, summaries off, and work waiting. Otherwise "Up to date".
 */
export function headline(status: IndexStatus): { tone: Tone; text: string } {
    for (const name of ORDER){
        if (status.stages[name].state === "running") return { tone: "busy", text: `${VERBS[name]}${progress(status.stages[name])}` };
    }
    for (const name of ORDER){
        if (status.stages[name].state === "failed") return { tone: "warn", text: `${TITLES[name]} failed` };
    }
    if (status.stages.structure.state !== "ready") return { tone: "warn", text: "Not indexed yet" };
    if (status.stages.summaries.state === "off") return { tone: "off", text: "Summaries off (no API key)" };
    for (const name of ORDER){
        const stage = status.stages[name];
        if (stage.state === "idle" && stage.total > stage.done) return { tone: "busy", text: `${TITLES[name]}${progress(stage)}` };
    }
    return { tone: "ok", text: "Up to date" };
}
```

```ts
import type { SummaryStatus } from "@/lib/contracts";

/** Whether opening a target should ask for its summary now (PIVOT_SPEC.md §5.3): it's missing or out of date. "off" never asks. */
export function wantsSummary(status: SummaryStatus): boolean {
    return status === "pending" || status === "stale";
}

/** What the panel says in place of a summary it doesn't have. */
export function placeholderFor(status: SummaryStatus): string {
    switch (status){
        case "off": return "Summaries are off: ANTHROPIC_API_KEY isn't set.";
        case "failed": return "The last attempt to summarize this failed.";
        case "pending": return "Summarizing…";
        default: return "";
    }
}
```
  7. `app/map/pulses.ts`:
```ts
/** A box pulses at most once per this long, however many tool calls touch it (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7: throttled). */
export const PULSE_GAP_MS = 800;

export type Pulse = { key: number; at: number };

/**
 * The pulse state after tool calls touched `boxes` at `now`. A box that hasn't pulsed in the last PULSE_GAP_MS gets a
 * new key, which remounts its pulse ring and plays it again; the others keep theirs.
 */
export function nextPulses(state: ReadonlyMap<string, Pulse>, boxes: readonly string[], now: number): Map<string, Pulse> {
    const next = new Map(state);
    for (const box of new Set(boxes)){
        const last = next.get(box);
        if (last && now - last.at < PULSE_GAP_MS) continue;
        next.set(box, { key: (last?.key ?? 0) + 1, at: now });
    }
    return next;
}
```
  8. `app/map/perf.ts` (browser-only, so untested here; Task 22 reads what it records):
```ts
/**
 * Interaction timings for S3 (PIVOT_SPEC.md §2: click, zoom or level change → rendered ≤ 100 ms). A start mark goes
 * down when the interaction begins, and the measure is taken after the frame that painted its result. Task 22 reads
 * them from window.__nebulosityPerf.
 */
type PerfWindow = Window & { __nebulosityPerf?: { name: string; ms: number }[] };

export function markStart(name: string): void {
    performance.clearMarks(`${name}:start`);
    performance.mark(`${name}:start`);
}

export function measureAfterPaint(name: string): void {
    // the first frame callback runs before the paint that shows the change; the second runs after it
    requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        if (performance.getEntriesByName(`${name}:start`, "mark").length === 0) return;
        const measure = performance.measure(name, `${name}:start`);
        performance.clearMarks(`${name}:start`);
        const w = window as PerfWindow;
        (w.__nebulosityPerf ??= []).push({ name, ms: Math.round(measure.duration * 10) / 10 });
    }));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: The status poll** — `app/map/useIndexStatus.ts`:
```ts
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { IndexStatus } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { api, ApiError } from "./api";

/** How often the Map asks for the index status: the pill reads it, and `indexVersion` says when to refetch. */
export const STATUS_POLL_MS = 2_000;

/**
 * The active repo's index status, polled every 2 s and whenever the window regains focus. `error` is set while there's
 * no repo to show (INDEX_EMPTY) or the server can't be reached, and the last good status stays meanwhile.
 */
export function useIndexStatus(): { status: IndexStatus | null; error: ApiError | null; refresh: () => void } {
    const [status, setStatus] = useState<IndexStatus | null>(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);
    const [tick, setTick] = useState(0);

    useEffect(() => {
        const controller = new AbortController();
        api.status(controller.signal).then(
            (next) => { setStatus(next); setError(null); },
            (err: unknown) => { if (err instanceof ApiError) setError(err); },
        );
        const timer = setTimeout(() => setTick((t) => t + 1), STATUS_POLL_MS);
        return () => {
            controller.abort();
            clearTimeout(timer);
        };
    }, [tick]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const onFocus = () => setTick((t) => t + 1);
        window.addEventListener("focus", onFocus);
        return () => window.removeEventListener("focus", onFocus);
    }, []);

    const refresh = useCallback(() => setTick((t) => t + 1), []);
    return { status, error, refresh };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: A box** — `app/map/MapBox.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { memo } from "react";
import { Handle, Position, type Node, type NodeProps } from "@xyflow/react";
import type { MapNode } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { formatCount } from "./format";

/** What a box carries besides React Flow's own fields. */
export type BoxData = {
    box: MapNode;
    selected: boolean;
    highlighted: boolean;
    /** Changes when a tool call touches this box: the pulse ring remounts and plays again. */
    pulseKey: number;
};

export type BoxNode = Node<BoxData, "box">;

const PLACEHOLDERS: Partial<Record<MapNode["summaryStatus"], string>> = {
    pending: "summary pending",
    failed: "summary failed",
    off: "no summary (no API key)",
};

/**
 * One box on the Map (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): its name, how many files it holds, and its tagline, or a quiet placeholder
 * while there's none. An out-of-date tagline is dimmed. "+N more" is drawn dashed; selecting it lists what it folds.
 */
function MapBox({ data }: NodeProps<BoxNode>){
    const { box, selected, highlighted, pulseKey } = data;
    const more = box.kind === "more";
    const border = selected ? "border-purple-300 shadow-[0_0_18px_rgba(168,85,247,0.55)]"
        : highlighted ? "border-cyan-300/80 shadow-[0_0_14px_rgba(34,211,238,0.35)]"
        : more ? "border-dashed border-purple-400/35"
        : "border-purple-400/25";
    const current = Boolean(box.tagline) && box.summaryStatus !== "stale";

    return (
        <div
            data-box={box.path}
            title={box.path || box.name}
            className={`relative h-[76px] w-[224px] rounded-sm border bg-black/60 px-3 py-2 font-mono backdrop-blur-md transition-colors ${border}`}
        >
            {pulseKey > 0 && <span key={pulseKey} aria-hidden className="neb-pulse pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 rounded-sm" />}
            <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                <span className={`truncate text-[12px] ${more ? "text-purple-200/80" : "text-zinc-100"}`}>
                    {box.name}{box.kind === "folder" ? "/" : ""}
                </span>
                <span className="shrink-0 text-[10px] text-zinc-500">{box.kind === "file" ? "file" : `${formatCount(box.fileCount)} files`}</span>
            </div>
            <p className={`mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-[10.5px] leading-snug ${current ? "text-zinc-400" : "italic text-zinc-600"}`}>
                {more ? "select to list them" : box.tagline ?? PLACEHOLDERS[box.summaryStatus] ?? ""}
            </p>
            <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} className="!h-1 !w-1 !border-0 !bg-purple-400/40" />
            <Handle type="source" position={Position.Bottom} className="!h-1 !w-1 !border-0 !bg-purple-400/40" />
        </div>
    );
}

// memo: React Flow re-renders nodes on every pan and zoom otherwise
export default memo(MapBox);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The canvas** — `app/map/MapCanvas.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
    Background, BackgroundVariant, Controls, MarkerType, ReactFlow, useEdgesState, useNodesState, useReactFlow,
    type Edge, type EdgeMouseHandler, type NodeMouseHandler, type NodeTypes,
} from "@xyflow/react";
import "@xyflow/react/dist/style.css";
import type { LevelResponse } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { edgeOpacity, edgeWidth } from "./edges";
import { formatCount } from "./format";
import { BOX_HEIGHT, BOX_WIDTH, layoutLevel } from "./layout";
import MapBox, { type BoxNode } from "./MapBox";
import type { Pulse } from "./pulses";

// at module scope: a new object per render would make React Flow remount every box
const nodeTypes: NodeTypes = { box: MapBox };

type MapCanvasProps = {
    level: LevelResponse;
    selected: string | null;
    /** Boxes a show_on_map highlight points at (Task 20). */
    highlighted: ReadonlySet<string>;
    pulses: ReadonlyMap<string, Pulse>;
    onSelect: (path: string | null) => void;
    onOpen: (folder: string) => void;
    /** Called once a new level is on screen (S3's measuring point). */
    onDrawn: () => void;
};

/**
 * The canvas (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): one level's boxes, laid out by dagre, and the lines between them. A click selects
 * and a double-click opens a folder. The selected box's lines are drawn at full strength, the rest ghosted, and
 * hovering a line shows its count. React Flow stays controlled with the StrictMode workaround in next.config.ts.
 */
export default function MapCanvas({ level, selected, highlighted, pulses, onSelect, onOpen, onDrawn }: MapCanvasProps){
    const positions = useMemo(() => layoutLevel(level.nodes, level.edges), [level]);
    const maxCount = useMemo(() => Math.max(0, ...level.edges.map((e) => e.count)), [level]);
    const [hovered, setHovered] = useState<string | null>(null);
    const [nodes, setNodes, onNodesChange] = useNodesState<BoxNode>([]);
    const [edges, setEdges, onEdgesChange] = useEdgesState<Edge>([]);
    const { fitView } = useReactFlow();

    useEffect(() => {
        setNodes(level.nodes.map((box) => ({
            id: box.path,
            type: "box" as const,
            position: positions.get(box.path) ?? { x: 0, y: 0 },
            width: BOX_WIDTH,
            height: BOX_HEIGHT,
            draggable: false,
            connectable: false,
            data: { box, selected: box.path === selected, highlighted: highlighted.has(box.path), pulseKey: pulses.get(box.path)?.key ?? 0 },
        })));
    }, [level, positions, selected, highlighted, pulses, setNodes]);

    useEffect(() => {
        setEdges(level.edges.map((edge) => {
            const id = `${edge.source}->${edge.target}`;
            const stroke = `rgba(168, 85, 247, ${edgeOpacity(edge, selected)})`;
            return {
                id,
                source: edge.source,
                target: edge.target,
                focusable: false,
                label: hovered === id ? `${formatCount(edge.count)} import${edge.count === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : undefined,
                labelStyle: { fill: "#e4e4e7", fontSize: 11 },
                labelBgStyle: { fill: "#0c0716", fillOpacity: 0.9 },
                style: { stroke, strokeWidth: edgeWidth(edge.count, maxCount) },
                markerEnd: { type: MarkerType.ArrowClosed, width: 14, height: 14, color: stroke },
            };
        }));
    }, [level, selected, hovered, maxCount, setEdges]);

    // a new level: fit it to the screen, then report it drawn
    useEffect(() => {
        const frame = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
            void fitView({ padding: 0.12, duration: 0 });
            onDrawn();
        });
        return () => cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
    }, [level, fitView, onDrawn]);

    const onNodeClick: NodeMouseHandler<BoxNode> = (_, node) => onSelect(node.id);
    const onNodeDoubleClick: NodeMouseHandler<BoxNode> = (_, node) => {
        if (node.data.box.kind === "folder") onOpen(node.id);
    };
    const onEdgeMouseEnter: EdgeMouseHandler = (_, edge) => setHovered(edge.id);
    const onEdgeMouseLeave: EdgeMouseHandler = () => setHovered(null);

    return (
        <ReactFlow
            nodes={nodes}
            edges={edges}
            onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
            onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
            nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
            onNodeClick={onNodeClick}
            onNodeDoubleClick={onNodeDoubleClick}
            onPaneClick={() => onSelect(null)}
            onEdgeMouseEnter={onEdgeMouseEnter}
            onEdgeMouseLeave={onEdgeMouseLeave}
            nodesDraggable={false}
            nodesConnectable={false}
            zoomOnDoubleClick={false}
            minZoom={0.1}
            maxZoom={2}
            colorMode="dark"
            className="!bg-transparent"
        >
            <Background bgColor="transparent" variant={BackgroundVariant.Lines} gap={48} lineWidth={0.5} color="rgba(157, 80, 221, 0.10)" />
            <Controls
                position="bottom-right"
                showInteractive={false}
                className="!rounded-none !border !border-purple-500/20 !bg-[#0c0716]/80 !shadow-none backdrop-blur-xl"
            />
        </ReactFlow>
    );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { nameOf } from "./paths";

type BreadcrumbProps = { repo: string; trail: readonly string[]; onOpen: (folder: string) => void };

/** Where the Map is: the repo, then each folder down to the one on screen. Every step but the last opens it. */
export default function Breadcrumb({ repo, trail, onOpen }: BreadcrumbProps){
    return (
        <nav aria-label="Where you are" className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
            {trail.map((step, i) => (
                <span key={step || "(root)"} className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1">
                    {i > 0 && <span aria-hidden className="text-zinc-600">›</span>}
                    {i === trail.length - 1
                        ? <span aria-current="page" className="truncate text-zinc-100">{nameOf(step, repo)}</span>
                        : <button type="button" onClick={() => onOpen(step)} className="truncate hover:text-purple-200">{nameOf(step, repo)}</button>}
                </span>
            ))}
        </nav>
    );
}
```

```tsx
"use client";

import { useSyncExternalStore, type ReactNode } from "react";

type TopBarProps = {
    /** The repo's name, or the repo picker (Task 20). */
    repo: ReactNode;
    /** Opens ⌘K search (Task 19). The button appears once it's passed. */
    onSearch?: () => void;
    /** The index-status pill (Task 20). */
    status?: ReactNode;
};

const noSubscription = () => () => {};

/** The top bar (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): the repo, the search palette's button, and a lens switcher showing the lenses that exist. */
export default function TopBar({ repo, onSearch, status }: TopBarProps){
    // the server renders "Ctrl K"; a Mac shows "⌘K" once hydrated, without a mismatch
    const isMac = useSyncExternalStore(noSubscription, () => /Mac|iPhone|iPad/.test(navigator.platform), () => false);

    return (
        <header className="pointer-events-auto relative z-20 flex h-11 items-center gap-4 border-b border-purple-500/15 bg-[#06070B]/70 px-4 backdrop-blur-xl">
            <div className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
                <span aria-hidden className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-300/80" />
                {repo}
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-1 justify-center">
                {onSearch && (
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={onSearch}
                        data-testid="search-button"
                        className="flex w-full max-w-md items-center justify-between rounded-sm border border-purple-400/20 bg-black/40 px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-500 hover:border-purple-300/40 hover:text-zinc-300"
                    >
                        <span>Search files, symbols and summaries…</span>
                        <kbd className="text-[10px] text-zinc-600">{isMac ? "⌘K" : "Ctrl K"}</kbd>
                    </button>
                )}
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">

                {/* the lens switcher: Structure is v1's only lens (History arrives with E3, Clusters with E5) */}
                <span className="rounded-sm border border-purple-400/30 bg-purple-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-purple-200">
                    Structure
                </span>
            </div>
        </header>
    );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The Map** — `app/map/MapView.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ReactFlowProvider } from "@xyflow/react";
import type { LevelResponse } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { api, ApiError } from "./api";
import Breadcrumb from "./Breadcrumb";
import MapCanvas from "./MapCanvas";
import { parentOf } from "./paths";
import { markStart, measureAfterPaint } from "./perf";
import type { Pulse } from "./pulses";
import TopBar from "./TopBar";
import { useIndexStatus } from "./useIndexStatus";

/** Levels fetched for one repo and index version (ADR-036): going back up a level is instant until the index moves. */
type LevelCache = { repo: string; version: number; levels: ReadonlyMap<string, LevelResponse> };

const NO_PULSES: ReadonlyMap<string, Pulse> = new Map();

/** The Map (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): the page's one client component. */
export default function MapView(){
    return (
        <ReactFlowProvider>
            <MapScreen />
        </ReactFlowProvider>
    );
}

function MapScreen(){
    const { status, error: statusError } = useIndexStatus();
    const [levelPath, setLevelPath] = useState("");
    const [cache, setCache] = useState<LevelCache | null>(null);
    const [levelError, setLevelError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);
    const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);

    const repo = status?.repo ?? null;
    const version = status?.indexVersion ?? null;
    // the level on screen: the one asked for, from this repo. While a newer version loads, the older copy stays up.
    const level = cache !== null && cache.repo === repo ? cache.levels.get(levelPath) ?? null : null;
    const current = cache !== null && cache.repo === repo && cache.version === version && cache.levels.has(levelPath);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (repo === null || version === null || current) return;
        const controller = new AbortController();
        api.level(levelPath, controller.signal).then(
            (next) => {
                setCache((c) => ({
                    repo: next.repo,
                    version: next.indexVersion,
                    levels: new Map(c !== null && c.repo === next.repo && c.version === next.indexVersion ? c.levels : []).set(levelPath, next),
                }));
                setLevelError(null);
            },
            (err: unknown) => { if (err instanceof ApiError) setLevelError(err); },
        );
        return () => controller.abort();
    }, [repo, version, levelPath, current]);

    /** Opens a folder's level, the Map's main move. */
    const open = useCallback((folder: string) => {
        markStart("map:level");
        setLevelPath(folder);
        setSelected(null);
    }, []);

    const select = useCallback((target: string | null) => {
        if (target !== null) markStart("map:select");
        setSelected(target);
    }, []);

    const onDrawn = useCallback(() => measureAfterPaint("map:level"), []);

    // Escape lets go of the selection, Backspace goes up a level, unless the user is typing
    useEffect(() => {
        const onKey = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
            const typing = event.target instanceof HTMLElement && ["INPUT", "TEXTAREA"].includes(event.target.tagName);
            if (typing) return;
            if (event.key === "Escape") setSelected(null);
            else if (event.key === "Backspace" && levelPath !== "") open(parentOf(levelPath));
        };
        window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
        return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    }, [levelPath, open]);

    if (statusError?.code === "INDEX_EMPTY") return <EmptyState />;

    return (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 flex flex-col">
            <TopBar repo={<span className="truncate font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-zinc-300">{repo ? repo.slice(repo.lastIndexOf("/") + 1) : "…"}</span>} />
            <div className="pointer-events-auto relative z-10 flex h-9 items-center justify-between gap-4 border-b border-purple-500/10 bg-[#06070B]/40 px-4 backdrop-blur-md">
                {level && repo ? <Breadcrumb repo={repo} trail={level.breadcrumb} onOpen={open} /> : <span />}
                {level && level.hiddenCount > 0 && (
                    <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[10px] text-zinc-500">
                        {level.nodes.length - 1} of {level.nodes.length - 1 + level.hiddenCount} shown
                    </span>
                )}
            </div>
            <main className="relative flex-1">
                {level && (
                    <MapCanvas level={level} selected={selected} highlighted={new Set()} pulses={NO_PULSES} onSelect={select} onOpen={open} onDrawn={onDrawn} />
                )}
                {!level && !levelError && <p className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center font-mono text-xs text-zinc-500">Loading the map…</p>}
                {levelError && <p className="absolute inset-x-0 top-6 text-center font-mono text-xs text-red-300">{levelError.message}</p>}
            </main>
        </div>
    );
}

function EmptyState(){
    return (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center">
            <div className="max-w-md space-y-2 text-center font-mono text-xs text-zinc-400">
                <p className="text-zinc-200">No repository indexed yet.</p>
                <p>Index one from a terminal, then reload: <code className="text-purple-200">npm run index -- &lt;path-to-repo&gt;</code></p>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
```
  `highlighted={new Set()}` makes a new Set every render, which re-runs the canvas's node effect. Task 20 replaces it with a memoized set; until then the cost is a few microseconds.

```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import type { AreaCount, DescribeResponse, LevelResponse, MapNode, SummarizeResponse, SymbolInfo } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { api, ApiError } from "./api";
import { formatCount } from "./format";
import { nameOf } from "./paths";
import { measureAfterPaint } from "./perf";
import { placeholderFor, wantsSummary } from "./summaries";

type DetailPanelProps = {
    /** The selection: a file, a folder, the repo, or the level's "+N more" box. */
    path: string;
    level: LevelResponse;
    /** Moves when the index changes; the panel then refetches. */
    indexVersion: number;
    /** Go to the level holding a path and select it there. */
    onFocus: (targetPath: string) => void;
    onOpen: (folder: string) => void;
    onClose: () => void;
};

/** Long lists show this many at a time (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7: "20 entries plus load more"). */
const PAGE = 20;

/**
 * The detail panel (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7). It opens on selection with what describe() knows: the summary, the size, what's
 * inside (children or symbols), what it uses and what uses it. A missing or out-of-date summary is asked for once
 * ("summarize now", §5.3). Selecting "+N more" lists the boxes it folds. MapView remounts the panel for each
 * selection (key), so nothing leaks from one target to the next.
 */
export default function DetailPanel({ path, level, indexVersion, onFocus, onOpen, onClose }: DetailPanelProps){
    const more = level.nodes.find((n) => n.path === path && n.kind === "more") ?? null;
    const isMore = more !== null;
    const target = isMore ? level.path : path;
    const [data, setData] = useState<DescribeResponse | null>(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);
    const [fresh, setFresh] = useState<SummarizeResponse | null>(null);
    const [summaryError, setSummaryError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);
    const asked = useRef(new Set<string>());

    useEffect(() => {
        const controller = new AbortController();
        api.describe(target, controller.signal).then(
            (next) => { setData(next); setError(null); },
            (err: unknown) => { if (err instanceof ApiError) setError(err); },
        );
        return () => controller.abort();
    }, [target, indexVersion]);

    useEffect(() => {
        if (data) measureAfterPaint("map:select");
    }, [data]);

    // "summarize now", once per target and index version: the server joins a request already running (ADR-033)
    useEffect(() => {
        if (data === null || isMore || !wantsSummary(data.summaryStatus)) return;
        const key = `${data.path}@${data.indexVersion}`;
        if (asked.current.has(key)) return;
        asked.current.add(key);
        api.summarize(data.path).then(setFresh, (err: unknown) => { if (err instanceof ApiError) setSummaryError(err); });
    }, [data, isMore]);

    const retry = (): void => {
        if (data === null) return;
        setSummaryError(null);
        api.summarize(data.path).then(setFresh, (err: unknown) => { if (err instanceof ApiError) setSummaryError(err); });
    };

    const name = more ? more.name : nameOf(target, level.repo);
    const kindLabel = more ? "folded here" : data?.kind ?? "";

    return (
        <aside data-panel={path} className="pointer-events-auto absolute inset-y-0 right-0 z-10 flex w-[380px] flex-col border-l border-purple-400/20 bg-[#0c0716]/85 font-mono backdrop-blur-xl">
            <header className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 border-b border-purple-400/15 px-4 py-3">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-zinc-500">{kindLabel}</div>
                    <h2 className="truncate text-[13px] text-zinc-100">{name}</h2>
                    <div className="truncate text-[10px] text-zinc-500">{target || "(repo root)"}</div>
                </div>
                <button type="button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close the panel" className="text-lg leading-none text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-200">×</button>
            </header>
            <div className="flex-1 space-y-5 overflow-y-auto px-4 py-4 text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-zinc-300">
                {error && <p className="text-red-300">{error.message}</p>}
                {!data && !error && <p className="text-zinc-500">Loading…</p>}
                {data && more && <FoldedList data={data} level={level} onFocus={onFocus} />}
                {data && !more && (
                    <Details data={data} fresh={fresh} summaryError={summaryError} onRetry={retry} onFocus={onFocus} onOpen={onOpen} />
                )}
            </div>
        </aside>
    );
}

type DetailsProps = {
    data: DescribeResponse;
    fresh: SummarizeResponse | null;
    summaryError: ApiError | null;
    onRetry: () => void;
    onFocus: (targetPath: string) => void;
    onOpen: (folder: string) => void;
};

function Details({ data, fresh, summaryError, onRetry, onFocus, onOpen }: DetailsProps){
    // a "summarize now" answer is newer than the description it was asked from
    const shown = fresh ?? data;
    const editorLink = (line?: number) => `vscode://file/${data.repo}/${data.path}${line ? `:${line}` : ""}`;

    return (
        <>
            <section>
                {shown.tagline
                    ? <p className={`text-[12.5px] ${shown.summaryStatus === "stale" ? "text-zinc-400" : "text-zinc-100"}`}>{shown.tagline}</p>
                    : <p className="italic text-zinc-500">{summaryError?.code === "NOT_READY" ? "Waiting for what's inside to be summarized first." : placeholderFor(shown.summaryStatus)}</p>}
                {shown.summary && <p className="mt-1.5 text-zinc-300">{shown.summary}</p>}
                {shown.summaryStatus === "stale" && <p className="mt-1.5 text-[10.5px] text-amber-200/70">Out of date: this changed since it was summarized.</p>}
                {(shown.summaryError ?? (summaryError && summaryError.code !== "NOT_READY" ? summaryError.message : null)) && (
                    <p className="mt-1.5 text-[10.5px] text-red-300">
                        {shown.summaryError ?? summaryError?.message}{" "}
                        <button type="button" onClick={onRetry} className="underline hover:text-red-200">Try again</button>
                    </p>
                )}
            </section>

            <section className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-[10.5px] text-zinc-500">
                <span>{formatCount(data.stats.fileCount)} {data.stats.fileCount === 1 ? "file" : "files"} · {formatCount(data.stats.lineCount)} lines</span>
                {data.stalePaths > 0 && <span className="text-amber-200/70">{formatCount(data.stalePaths)} changed since summarized</span>}
                {data.kind === "folder" && <button type="button" onClick={() => onOpen(data.path)} className="text-purple-300/80 hover:text-purple-200">Open folder</button>}
                {data.kind === "file" && <a href={editorLink()} className="text-purple-300/80 hover:text-purple-200">Open in VS Code</a>}
            </section>

            {data.children && (
                <List title="Inside" items={data.children} render={(child: MapNode) => (
                    <li key={child.path}>
                        <button type="button" onClick={() => onFocus(child.path)} className="w-full text-left hover:text-purple-200">
                            <span className="text-zinc-200">{child.name}{child.kind === "folder" ? "/" : ""}</span>
                            <span className="text-zinc-600"> · {child.kind === "folder" ? `${formatCount(child.fileCount)} files` : "file"}</span>
                            {child.tagline && <span className="block truncate text-[10.5px] text-zinc-500">{child.tagline}</span>}
                        </button>
                    </li>
                )} />
            )}

            {data.symbols && (
                <List title="Declarations" items={data.symbols} empty="None found." render={(symbol: SymbolInfo) => (
                    <li key={`${symbol.name}:${symbol.startLine}`}>
                        <a href={editorLink(symbol.startLine)} className="hover:text-purple-200">
                            <span className={symbol.exported ? "text-zinc-200" : "text-zinc-400"}>{symbol.name}</span>
                            <span className="text-zinc-600"> · {symbol.kind} · line {symbol.startLine}</span>
                        </a>
                    </li>
                )} />
            )}

            <List title="Uses" items={data.uses} empty="Imports nothing outside itself." render={(area: AreaCount) => <AreaRow key={area.path} area={area} onFocus={onFocus} />} />
            <List title="Used by" items={data.usedBy} empty="Nothing outside it imports it." render={(area: AreaCount) => <AreaRow key={area.path} area={area} onFocus={onFocus} />} />
        </>
    );
}

function AreaRow({ area, onFocus }: { area: AreaCount; onFocus: (targetPath: string) => void }){
    return (
        <li>
            <button type="button" onClick={() => onFocus(area.path)} className="flex w-full justify-between gap-3 text-left hover:text-purple-200">
                <span className="truncate">{area.path}{area.kind === "folder" ? "/" : ""}</span>
                <span className="shrink-0 text-zinc-500">{formatCount(area.count)}</span>
            </button>
        </li>
    );
}

/** "+N more" selected: the boxes it folds, from describe(level).children minus the ones drawn. */
function FoldedList({ data, level, onFocus }: { data: DescribeResponse; level: LevelResponse; onFocus: (targetPath: string) => void }){
    const drawn = new Set(level.nodes.map((n) => n.path));
    const folded = (data.children ?? []).filter((child) => !drawn.has(child.path));
    return (
        <List title="Folded into this box" items={folded} render={(child: MapNode) => (
            <li key={child.path}>
                <button type="button" onClick={() => onFocus(child.path)} className="w-full text-left hover:text-purple-200">
                    <span className="text-zinc-200">{child.name}{child.kind === "folder" ? "/" : ""}</span>
                    {child.tagline && <span className="block truncate text-[10.5px] text-zinc-500">{child.tagline}</span>}
                </button>
            </li>
        )} />
    );
}

type ListProps<T> = { title: string; items: readonly T[]; render: (item: T) => ReactNode; empty?: string };

/** A titled list that shows PAGE items at a time. */
function List<T>({ title, items, render, empty }: ListProps<T>){
    const [shown, setShown] = useState(PAGE);
    if (items.length === 0 && empty === undefined) return null;

    return (
        <section>
            <h3 className="mb-1.5 text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-zinc-500">
                {title} <span className="text-zinc-600">{formatCount(items.length)}</span>
            </h3>
            {items.length === 0
                ? <p className="text-zinc-600">{empty}</p>
                : <ul className="space-y-1">{items.slice(0, shown).map(render)}</ul>}
            {items.length > shown && (
                <button type="button" onClick={() => setShown((n) => n + PAGE)} className="mt-1.5 text-[10.5px] text-purple-300/80 hover:text-purple-200">
                    Show {Math.min(PAGE, items.length - shown)} more of {formatCount(items.length - shown)}
                </button>
            )}
        </section>
    );
}
```

### Task 19: ⌘K search

**Files:**
- Create: `app/map/SearchPalette.tsx`
- Modify: `app/map/MapView.tsx`

**Interfaces:** `<SearchPalette onPick(hit) onClose />`. Picking calls Task 18's `focus(hit.path)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: The palette** — `app/map/SearchPalette.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type KeyboardEvent } from "react";
import type { SearchHit, SearchResult } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { api, ApiError } from "./api";

type SearchPaletteProps = { onPick: (hit: SearchHit) => void; onClose: () => void };

/** Results asked for per query, and how long typing must pause before a query goes out. */
const LIMIT = 12;
const DEBOUNCE_MS = 150;

/**
 * ⌘K (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): hybrid search over files, symbols and summaries (GET /api/search). Arrow keys move, Enter
 * picks, Escape closes. Picking moves the Map to the result's level and selects it. MapView mounts it only while
 * open, so each opening starts empty.
 */
export default function SearchPalette({ onPick, onClose }: SearchPaletteProps){
    const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
    const [result, setResult] = useState<SearchResult | null>(null);
    const [error, setError] = useState<ApiError | null>(null);
    const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
    const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        input.current?.focus();
    }, []);

    useEffect(() => {
        const q = query.trim();
        if (q === "") return;
        const controller = new AbortController();
        const timer = setTimeout(() => {
            api.search(q, LIMIT, controller.signal).then(
                (next) => { setResult(next); setError(null); setActive(0); },
                (err: unknown) => { if (err instanceof ApiError) setError(err); },
            );
        }, DEBOUNCE_MS);
        return () => {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            controller.abort();
        };
    }, [query]);

    // results for an older query, or for an empty box, show nothing
    const hits = result !== null && result.query === query.trim() ? result.hits : [];
    const current = Math.min(active, hits.length - 1);

    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>): void => {
        if (event.key === "Escape") onClose();
        else if (event.key === "ArrowDown"){
            event.preventDefault();
            setActive(Math.min(current + 1, hits.length - 1));
        } else if (event.key === "ArrowUp"){
            event.preventDefault();
            setActive(Math.max(current - 1, 0));
        } else if (event.key === "Enter" && hits[current]) onPick(hits[current]);
    };

    return (
        <div className="pointer-events-auto fixed inset-0 z-30 bg-black/50 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
            <div role="dialog" aria-label="Search" onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
                className="mx-auto mt-[12vh] w-[600px] max-w-[92vw] overflow-hidden rounded-sm border border-purple-400/30 bg-[#0c0716]/95 font-mono shadow-[0_0_40px_rgba(126,58,242,0.25)]">
                <input
                    ref={input}
                    value={query}
                    onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
                    placeholder="Search files, symbols and summaries…"
                    spellCheck={false}
                    aria-label="Search"
                    className="w-full border-b border-purple-400/20 bg-transparent px-4 py-3 text-[13px] text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-600 focus:outline-none"
                />
                {error && <p className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-red-300">{error.message}</p>}
                {result?.note && hits.length > 0 && <p className="px-4 pt-2 text-[10.5px] text-amber-200/70">{result.note}</p>}
                <ul role="listbox" className="max-h-[52vh] overflow-y-auto py-1">
                    {hits.map((hit, i) => (
                        <li key={hit.path} role="option" aria-selected={i === current}>
                            <button
                                type="button"
                                data-hit={hit.path}
                                onMouseEnter={() => setActive(i)}
                                onClick={() => onPick(hit)}
                                className={`block w-full px-4 py-2 text-left ${i === current ? "bg-purple-500/15" : ""}`}
                            >
                                <span className="text-[12px] text-zinc-100">{hit.path}{hit.kind === "folder" ? "/" : ""}</span>
                                {hit.tagline && <span className="block truncate text-[10.5px] text-zinc-400">{hit.tagline}</span>}
                                {hit.code && (
                                    <span className="block truncate text-[10.5px] text-zinc-500">
                                        {hit.code.symbol} · lines {hit.code.startLine}–{hit.code.endLine}: {hit.code.snippet}
                                    </span>
                                )}
                            </button>
                        </li>
                    ))}
                    {query.trim() !== "" && result !== null && result.query === query.trim() && hits.length === 0 && (
                        <li className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] text-zinc-500">{result.note ?? "Nothing matched."}</li>
                    )}
                </ul>
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
```

### Task 20: The status pill, the live dock and the repo picker

**Files:**
- Create: `app/map/StatusPill.tsx`, `app/map/LiveDock.tsx`, `app/map/useLive.ts`, `app/map/RepoMenu.tsx`
- Modify: `app/map/MapView.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `useLive(onEvent): { last: LiveEvent | null; connected: boolean }`;
  - `<StatusPill status />`;
  - `<LiveDock last connected />`;
  - `<RepoMenu repo onSwitched />`.
- `MapView` gains live pulses, `show_on_map` highlights and focus, and the picker.

- [ ] **Step 1: The components.**
  1. `app/map/useLive.ts`:
```ts
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { LiveEvent } from "@/lib/contracts";

/**
 * The live channel (PIVOT_SPEC.md §8): every tool call from GET /api/live/stream. `onEvent` runs for each one (pulses,
 * UI actions), always the newest `onEvent` passed in; `last` is the newest event, for the dock. EventSource
 * reconnects by itself when the server restarts.
 */
export function useLive(onEvent: (event: LiveEvent) => void): { last: LiveEvent | null; connected: boolean } {
    const [last, setLast] = useState<LiveEvent | null>(null);
    const [connected, setConnected] = useState(false);
    const handler = useRef(onEvent);

    useEffect(() => {
        handler.current = onEvent;
    }, [onEvent]);

    useEffect(() => {
        const source = new EventSource("/api/live/stream");
        source.addEventListener("ready", () => setConnected(true));
        source.addEventListener("error", () => setConnected(false));
        source.addEventListener("tool", (message) => {
            const event = JSON.parse((message as MessageEvent<string>).data) as LiveEvent;
            setLast(event);
            handler.current(event);
        });
        return () => source.close();
    }, []);

    return { last, connected };
}
```
  2. `app/map/LiveDock.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { LiveEvent } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { ago } from "./format";

/** After this long without a tool call, the pill reads idle. */
const IDLE_AFTER_MS = 30_000;

/** The bottom dock (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): v1's live Claude Code pill. E1 adds the Guide's input beside it. */
export default function LiveDock({ last, connected }: { last: LiveEvent | null; connected: boolean }){
    // the clock lives in state, ticking every second: reading Date.now() during render isn't allowed
    const [now, setNow] = useState(0);
    useEffect(() => {
        const timer = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1_000);
        return () => clearInterval(timer);
    }, []);

    const age = last !== null && now > 0 ? now - Date.parse(last.at) : Infinity;
    const active = last !== null && age < IDLE_AFTER_MS;
    const dot = !connected ? "bg-zinc-600" : active ? "animate-pulse bg-cyan-300" : "bg-cyan-300/40";

    return (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-5 z-10 flex justify-center">
            <div data-testid="live-dock" className="pointer-events-auto flex max-w-[560px] items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-purple-400/25 bg-[#0c0716]/85 px-3 py-1 font-mono text-[10.5px] text-zinc-400 backdrop-blur-xl">
                <span aria-hidden className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full ${dot}`} />
                <span className="text-zinc-300">Claude Code</span>
                <span aria-hidden className="text-zinc-600">·</span>
                {active && last
                    ? (
                        <span className="truncate">
                            {last.tool} · {last.paths[0] || "the repo"}{last.paths.length > 1 ? ` +${last.paths.length - 1}` : ""} · {ago(age)}
                        </span>
                    )
                    : <span>{connected ? "idle" : "not connected"}</span>}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}
```
  3. `app/map/StatusPill.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import type { IndexStatus, StageName } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { api, ApiError } from "./api";
import { formatCount, headline, stageLabel, type Tone } from "./format";

const DOTS: Record<Tone, string> = {
    ok: "bg-green-300/80",
    busy: "animate-pulse bg-cyan-300/80",
    warn: "bg-amber-300/80",
    off: "bg-zinc-500",
};

const STAGES: readonly StageName[] = ["structure", "summaries", "embeddings"];

/**
 * The index-status pill (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7): the stage that most needs attention, in one line. Opening it lists every
 * stage with its counts and note, and offers a re-index (POST /api/index) with its progress.
 */
export default function StatusPill({ status }: { status: IndexStatus | null }){
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    const [reindex, setReindex] = useState<string | null>(null);
    if (status === null) return null;
    const head = headline(status);

    const runReindex = (): void => {
        setReindex("starting…");
        api.reindex((p) => setReindex(`${p.stage} ${formatCount(p.done)} / ${formatCount(p.total)}`)).then(
            (done) => setReindex(`re-indexed ${formatCount(done.files)} files in ${(done.totalMs / 1000).toFixed(1)} s`),
            (err: unknown) => setReindex(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "the re-index failed"),
        );
    };

    return (
        <div className="relative">
            <button
                type="button"
                data-testid="status-pill"
                onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full border border-purple-400/20 bg-black/40 px-2.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10.5px] text-zinc-300 hover:border-purple-300/40"
            >
                <span aria-hidden className={`h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full ${DOTS[head.tone]}`} />
                {head.text}
            </button>
            {open && (
                <div className="absolute right-0 top-8 z-30 w-[340px] space-y-2 rounded-sm border border-purple-400/25 bg-[#0c0716]/95 p-3 font-mono text-[10.5px] text-zinc-400 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(126,58,242,0.2)]">
                    {STAGES.map((name) => (
                        <div key={name}>
                            <div className="text-zinc-200">{stageLabel(name, status.stages[name])}</div>
                            {status.stages[name].note && <div className="text-zinc-500">{status.stages[name].note}</div>}
                        </div>
                    ))}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-purple-400/15 pt-2">
                        <span className="truncate text-zinc-500">{reindex ?? `index version ${status.indexVersion}`}</span>
                        <button type="button" onClick={runReindex} className="shrink-0 text-purple-300/80 hover:text-purple-200">Re-index</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}
```
  4. `app/map/RepoMenu.tsx`:
```tsx
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import type { RepoList } from "@/lib/contracts";
import { api, ApiError } from "./api";

type RepoMenuProps = { repo: string; onSwitched: () => void };

/** The repo's name, and a picker when more than one repo is indexed (PIVOT_SPEC.md §7.1). */
export default function RepoMenu({ repo, onSwitched }: RepoMenuProps){
    const [list, setList] = useState<RepoList | null>(null);
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
    const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

    useEffect(() => {
        api.repos().then(setList, () => {});
    }, [repo]);

    const name = repo.slice(repo.lastIndexOf("/") + 1);
    const label = <span className="truncate font-mono text-[11px] uppercase tracking-[0.3em] text-zinc-300">{name}</span>;
    if (list === null || list.repos.length < 2) return label;

    const pick = (root: string): void => {
        setOpen(false);
        if (root === repo) return;
        api.setActiveRepo(root).then(onSwitched, (err: unknown) => setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : "Couldn't switch repos."));
    };

    return (
        <div className="relative">
            <button type="button" data-testid="repo-menu" onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 hover:text-purple-200">
                {label}
                <span aria-hidden className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500">▾</span>
            </button>
            {open && (
                <ul className="absolute left-0 top-8 z-30 w-[320px] rounded-sm border border-purple-400/25 bg-[#0c0716]/95 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] shadow-[0_0_30px_rgba(126,58,242,0.2)]">
                    {list.repos.map((r) => (
                        <li key={r.root}>
                            <button type="button" onClick={() => pick(r.root)} className={`block w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-left hover:bg-purple-500/15 ${r.root === repo ? "text-purple-200" : "text-zinc-300"}`}>
                                {r.name}
                                <span className="block truncate text-[10px] text-zinc-600">{r.root}</span>
                            </button>
                        </li>
                    ))}
                </ul>
            )}
            {error && <span className="ml-2 text-[10px] text-red-300">{error}</span>}
        </div>
    );
}
```

```ts
/**
 * lib/contracts.ts — single source of truth for every shape shared between
 * the UI, the API adapters, and core services.
 *
 * This file is the compiler-enforced version of docs/API_CONTRACTS.md (and
 * the storage schemas in docs/DATABASE_DESIGN.md). If this file and those
 * docs ever disagree, fix the drift in the same commit — see the maintenance
 * rule in docs/ARCHITECTURE.md.
 *
 * Rules:
 *  - Types only. No functions, no logic, no runtime values (the one
 *    exception: CONTRACTS_VERSION).
 *  - Importable from anywhere (core, adapters, UI). Never imports from app/,
 *    and never a renderer's types: the UI maps these shapes onto React Flow
 *    itself (ADR-040).
 *  - Node ids, file and folder paths, tool arguments, and source citations
 *    are ALL repo-relative forward-slash paths ("src/vs/base/common/lifecycle.ts",
 *    "" for the repo root). One shared identifier is what lets every subsystem
 *    point at the same things.
 */

/**
 * The API contract's version (ADR-040): bumped on a breaking change to these
 * shapes. Stored data changes only through Drizzle migrations; nothing wipes
 * the database when this moves.
 */
export const CONTRACTS_VERSION = 2;
```

```ts
import { tool, type ToolRuntime } from "@langchain/core/tools";
import { ToolMessage } from "@langchain/core/messages";
import { Command } from "@langchain/langgraph";
import { z } from "zod";
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { readConfig } from "@/lib/config";
import { getDb } from "@/lib/db/client";
import { findRepository, loadImportEdgesFrom, loadImportEdgesTouching } from "@/lib/db/store/structure";
import { loadFileFacts, loadFolderFacts } from "@/lib/db/store/summaries";
import { search } from "@/lib/engine/search";
import { IGNORED_DIRS } from "@/lib/index/discover";
import { repoKey } from "@/lib/index/paths";

const MAX_READ_LINES = 400;

// V1.3: the tools answer about the active repo (.nebulosity/config.json) from the new engine. V1.4 moves their logic
// into lib/tools/registry.ts, and E1 wraps the registry's entries for the Guide (PIVOT_SPEC.md §6.2).

/** The repo the tools work on, and its id; null until one is indexed. */
function activeRepo(): { root: string; repoId: number } | null {
    const root = readConfig().activeRepo;
    if (root === null) return null;
    const repoId = findRepository(getDb(), repoKey(root));
    return repoId === null ? null : { root, repoId };
}

const NO_REPO = "ERROR INDEX_EMPTY: no repository is indexed yet — tell the user to run npm run index -- <path-to-repo>.";

// makes sure the relative path:
// 1. doesn't escape the repo (e.g., ../../../../etc/passwd)
// 2. doesn't touch ignored directories (e.g., .next, .git)
// 3. doesn't touch any secrets (e.g., .env)
function safePath(root: string, relativePath: string): { ok: true; absolute: string } | { ok: false; error: string } {
    const base = path.resolve(root);
    const resolved = path.resolve(base, relativePath);

    if (resolved !== base && !resolved.startsWith(base + path.sep)){
        return { ok: false, error: `Path "${relativePath}" resolves outside the repository.` };
    }

    const segments = path.relative(base, resolved).split(path.sep);

    if (segments.some((s) => IGNORED_DIRS.has(s) || s.startsWith(".env"))){
        return { ok: false, error: `Path "${relativePath}" is not accessible.` };
    }

    return { ok: true, absolute: resolved };
}
```
  Then, tool by tool, keep each wrapper's shape and change only what it reads:
  1. **`searchCode`.** Its body becomes:
```ts
    async ({ query }: { query: string }, runtime: ToolRuntime) => {
        const repo = activeRepo();
        if (!repo) return NO_REPO;

        const result = await search(repo.root, query, { limit: 8 });

        if (result.hits.length === 0){
            return `No results for "${query}". Try rewording the query once before concluding the code doesn't exist.${result.note ? ` (${result.note})` : ""}`;
        }

        // one line per file or folder: what it is, then its best-matching code when there is some
        const formatted = result.hits
            .map((h, i) => `[${i + 1}] ${h.path}${h.kind === "folder" ? "/" : ""} · ${h.tagline ?? "(no summary yet)"}`
                + (h.code ? `\n    ${h.code.symbol}, lines ${h.code.startLine}-${h.code.endLine}: ${h.code.snippet}` : ""))
            .join("\n");

        // cite every file this search actually surfaced
        return new Command({
            update: {
                messages: [new ToolMessage({ content: formatted, tool_call_id: runtime.toolCallId })],
                sources: result.hits.filter((h) => h.kind === "file").map((h) => h.path)
            }
        });
    },
```
     Its description becomes `"Hybrid search over the repository: code keywords, summary keywords and summary meaning. Use for any question about how something works or where something happens. Reword and retry if results look irrelevant."`.
  2. **`readFile` and `listDirectory`.**
     - Each body starts with `const repo = activeRepo(); if (!repo) return NO_REPO;`.
     - `safePath(relativePath)` becomes `safePath(repo.root, relativePath)`.
     - In `listDirectory`, `!IGNORE_LIST.includes(e.name)` becomes `!IGNORED_DIRS.has(e.name)`.
  3. **`getDependencies`.** Its body becomes:
```ts
    ({ path: filePath }) => {
        const repo = activeRepo();
        if (!repo) return NO_REPO;

        const edges = loadImportEdgesFrom(getDb(), repo.repoId, [filePath]);

        if (edges.length === 0){
            return `"${filePath}" has no outgoing dependencies (or the path isn't indexed).`;
        }

        return edges.map((e) => `${e.target} (via "${e.specifier}")`).join("\n");
    },
```

  5. **`getCentralFilesTool`.** Its body becomes:

```ts
    ({ limit }) => {
        const repo = activeRepo();
        if (!repo) return NO_REPO;

            .sort((a, b) => b.importedBy - a.importedBy || (a.path < b.path ? -1 : 1))
            .slice(0, limit ?? 10);

        if (central.length === 0){
            return "The index holds no files yet.";
        }

        return central
            .map((f, i) => `${i + 1}. ${f.path} (imported by ${f.importedBy})`)
            .join("\n");
    },
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Document it.**
  1. **ADR-040**, appended to `docs/ENGINEERING_DECISIONS.md`:
```md

## ADR-040 — Renderer-agnostic contracts (CONTRACTS_VERSION 2): the version names the API, migrations carry the data (‹today›)

**Context:** `lib/contracts.ts` imported React Flow's `Node` and `Edge` for the old graph (`GraphResponse`), which tied the API to one renderer (PIVOT_SPEC.md §3, rule 3). V1.3 retires that graph, its routes and the Phase 2–4 chunk pipeline. The spec also said "a schema version mismatch triggers a rebuild": `meta.schemaVersion` was meant to wipe derived data on a contract change. Nothing ever implemented it, and the database now holds summaries that cost money to rebuild ($10.30 for VS Code).
**Decision:**
- **Data, not drawing.** `lib/contracts.ts` describes data: `LevelResponse`, `MapNode`, `MapEdge`, `DescribeResponse`, `SummarizeResponse`, `SearchResult`, `IndexStatus`, `LiveEvent`, `RepoList` and `IndexEventMap`. The UI maps them onto React Flow itself.
- **Retired types.** `GraphResponse`, `HealthResponse`, `FileNodeData`, `GraphFileNode`, `FileKind`, `RetrievedChunk` and `SearchResponse` are deleted, with `GET /api/graph`, `GET /api/health`, the radial graph UI, `lib/graph.ts`, `lib/scan.ts`, `lib/retrieve.ts`, `lib/index-repo.ts`, `lib/index-status.ts` and `lib/db/graph-store.ts`.
- **The version.** `CONTRACTS_VERSION` is 2. It names the HTTP and SSE contract, and moves on a breaking change to it; it never touches the database.
- **The data.** Stored data changes only through Drizzle migrations, which carry their own data fixes. 0004 cleared content hashes to force a re-parse; 0005 drops the chunk `vectors` table, whose last writer was `lib/index-repo.ts`. Nothing wipes the database on a version change. Summaries are keyed by path, so they survive any structure rebuild anyway.
- **The tools.** `lib/agent/tools.ts`, the owner's Phase 5 tools, is ported onto the new engine as-is. V1.4 moves its logic into the tool registry.
**Alternatives:** (a) Implement the wipe-and-rebuild: a contract change would throw away paid-for summaries for no gain. (b) Keep the React Flow types next to the new ones: the very coupling the adapter split exists to remove.
**Consequences:** A static export (V1.5), the MCP server (V1.4) and a desktop shell can all serve the same shapes. The regex scanner's golden vectors (`GRAPH_IMPLEMENTATION_REVIEW.md` PART 3) retire with the code they tested.
**Revisit when:** a second renderer (a WebGL overview, say) needs layout data the contract doesn't carry; then it becomes a separate, renderer-neutral shape.
```

### Task 22: Measure S3, S4 and S6 on VS Code

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/bench-map.ts`, `scripts/bench-update.ts`
- Modify: `package.json` (`"bench-map"`, `"bench-update"`)
- Docs: `docs/BENCHMARKS.md`

**Before you start:**
- Nothing else heavy may run: no `npm run summarize`, no other index run.
- VS Code is the active repo.

- [ ] **Step 1: The server-side benchmark** — `scripts/bench-map.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * npm run bench-map -- <path-to-repo>
 *
 * S3's server side and S4 (PIVOT_SPEC.md §2). It calls level() for every folder of an indexed repo, and describe() for
 * every folder and about 500 files, in this process (no HTTP), and prints p50, p95 and max for each. It also checks
 * that no level draws more than 50 boxes. It writes nothing.
 */
import path from "path";
import { getDb } from "../lib/db/client";
import { requireRepository } from "../lib/db/store/structure";
import { loadFileFacts, loadFolderFacts } from "../lib/db/store/summaries";
import { describe } from "../lib/engine/describe";
import { level, MAP_BOX_CAP } from "../lib/engine/level";
import { repoKey } from "../lib/index/paths";

const target = process.argv[2];
if (!target){
    console.error("usage: npm run bench-map -- <path-to-repo>");
    process.exit(1);
}

const root = path.resolve(target);
const db = getDb();
const repoId = requireRepository(db, repoKey(root));
const folders = loadFolderFacts(db, repoId).map((f) => f.path);
const files = loadFileFacts(db, repoId).map((f) => f.path);
const step = Math.max(1, Math.floor(files.length / 500));
const sample = files.filter((_, i) => i % step === 0);   // about 500, spread over the repo

const timed = (fn: () => void): number => {
    const started = performance.now();
    fn();
    return performance.now() - started;
};

function report(label: string, ms: readonly number[]): string {
    const sorted = [...ms].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const at = (q: number): number => sorted[Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.floor(q * sorted.length))];
    return `${label}: ${ms.length} calls · p50 ${at(0.5).toFixed(1)} ms · p95 ${at(0.95).toFixed(1)} ms · max ${sorted[sorted.length - 1].toFixed(1)} ms`;
}

// the first describe() loads the import graph, once per index version: timed on its own
const graphLoad = timed(() => describe(root, ""));

// level(): one pass to warm up, then the measured pass (level() caches nothing)
let widest = 0;
for (const folder of folders) widest = Math.max(widest, level(root, folder).nodes.length);
const levelMs = folders.map((folder) => timed(() => level(root, folder)));

// describe(): one pass each, the way a first click on each target costs (area counts are cached per path)
const folderMs = folders.map((folder) => timed(() => describe(root, folder)));
const fileMs = sample.map((file) => timed(() => describe(root, file)));

console.log(`[bench-map] ${root}: ${folders.length} folders, ${sample.length} of ${files.length} files`);
console.log(`[bench-map] first describe (loads the import graph): ${graphLoad.toFixed(0)} ms`);
console.log(`[bench-map] ${report("level", levelMs)}`);
console.log(`[bench-map] ${report("describe, folders", folderMs)}`);
console.log(`[bench-map] ${report("describe, files", fileMs)}`);
console.log(`[bench-map] most boxes on any level: ${widest} (S4, at most ${MAP_BOX_CAP}: ${widest <= MAP_BOX_CAP ? "PASS" : "FAIL"})`);
```
  Add `"bench-map": "tsx scripts/bench-map.ts"` to `package.json`, then run `npm run bench-map -- ../vscode`. Expected:
  - `level` p95 well under 50 ms (S3's level API);
  - most boxes on any level: 50 (S4 PASS).

- [ ] **Step 2: S6's benchmark, through the running server** — `scripts/bench-update.ts`:
```ts
/**
 * npm run bench-update -- <path-to-repo> <repo-relative file> [--runs 5]
 *
 * S6 (PIVOT_SPEC.md §2): the time from saving a file until describe() shows the change, through the running server
 * and its background worker, as a user sees it. Each run appends an export to the file and polls GET /api/describe
 * every 25 ms until the new symbol shows. It then restores the file's exact bytes and waits until the symbol is gone.
 * Needs npm run dev with that repo active. The file is always restored, and the script checks it was.
 */
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { readConfig } from "../lib/config";

const args = process.argv.slice(2);
const [target, rel] = args;
const runsAt = args.indexOf("--runs");
const runs = runsAt === -1 ? 5 : Number(args[runsAt + 1]);
if (!target || !rel || rel.startsWith("--") || !Number.isInteger(runs) || runs < 1){
    console.error("usage: npm run bench-update -- <path-to-repo> <repo-relative file> [--runs 5]");
    process.exit(1);
}

const file = path.join(path.resolve(target), rel);
const original = fs.readFileSync(file);
const url = `http://127.0.0.1:${readConfig().port}/api/describe?path=${encodeURIComponent(rel)}`;
const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));

async function hasSymbol(name: string): Promise<boolean> {
    const response = await fetch(url);
    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`describe answered ${response.status}: is npm run dev running with this repo active?`);
    const body = (await response.json()) as { symbols?: { name: string }[] };
    return (body.symbols ?? []).some((s) => s.name === name);
}

async function waitFor(name: string, present: boolean, limitMs: number): Promise<number> {
    const started = performance.now();
    while (performance.now() - started < limitMs){
        if ((await hasSymbol(name)) === present) return performance.now() - started;
        await sleep(25);
    }
    throw new Error(`gave up after ${limitMs} ms waiting for ${name} to ${present ? "appear" : "go"}`);
}

async function main(): Promise<void> {
    const timings: number[] = [];
    try{
        for (let i = 0; i < runs; i++){
            const name = `__nebulosityBench${i}`;
            fs.writeFileSync(file, Buffer.concat([original, Buffer.from(`\nexport const ${name} = ${i};\n`)]));
            timings.push(await waitFor(name, true, 30_000));
            fs.writeFileSync(file, original);
            await waitFor(name, false, 30_000);
            await sleep(500);
        }
    } finally{
        fs.writeFileSync(file, original);
    }
    if (!fs.readFileSync(file).equals(original)) throw new Error(`${rel} wasn't restored byte for byte`);

    const sorted = [...timings].sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const worst = sorted[sorted.length - 1];
    console.log(`[bench-update] ${rel}: save → describe shows it, ${runs} runs: ${timings.map((t) => `${Math.round(t)} ms`).join(", ")}`);
    console.log(`[bench-update] median ${Math.round(sorted[Math.floor(sorted.length / 2)])} ms · worst ${Math.round(worst)} ms · S6 (≤ 2,000 ms): ${worst <= 2_000 ? "PASS" : "FAIL"}`);
}

main().catch((err: unknown) => {
    console.error(err);
    process.exit(1);
});
```
  1. Add `"bench-update": "tsx scripts/bench-update.ts"` to `package.json`.
  2. Start the preview, and wait for `[background] watching …` in `preview_logs`.
  3. Run `npm run bench-update -- ../vscode src/vs/base/common/strings.ts`, then the same with `src/vs/editor/common/model/textModel.ts`, a 111,767-character file.
  4. Expected: every run under 2,000 ms. About 1 s of each is the debounce the spec asks for.
  5. `git -C ../vscode status --short` must print nothing.
  6. Each edit is undone within seconds, so the refresh 60 s later finds each summary current again and sends nothing.
  7. If S6 fails, find where the time goes before changing anything. The worker logs `updated N changed and M deleted files in X ms` for every update.
     - If `X` is the bulk, `loadStoredFiles` reading every file's specifiers is the likely cost. Replace it, in the edit-only path, with a query for the changed files' rows plus the path set. The tests must still pass unchanged.
     - If `X` is small, compare with `bench-map`'s "first describe" line: the first `describe()` after a version bump reloads the import graph. If that's the bulk, keep the graph cached across versions and patch it with the update's added and removed edges.
     - If neither, the time is in the watcher. Check whether chokidar fell back to polling: it logs nothing, so compare `fs.watch` timestamps in a throwaway script.

  3. **Clicks.** Click about ten boxes, files and folders.
  4. **Read the timings.** With `javascript_tool`:
```js
const byName = {};
for (const { name, ms } of window.__nebulosityPerf) (byName[name] ??= []).push(ms);
Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(byName).map(([name, ms]) => {
  const s = [...ms].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  return [name, { n: s.length, p50: s[Math.floor(s.length / 2)], p95: s[Math.min(s.length - 1, Math.floor(s.length * 0.95))], max: s[s.length - 1] }];
}))
```
  5. **Zoom.** Run this with `javascript_tool`: 20 wheel events over the canvas while recording frame intervals.
```js
await new Promise((resolve) => {
  const pane = document.querySelector(".react-flow__pane");
  const frames = [];
  let last = performance.now();
  let n = 0;
  const tick = (t) => { frames.push(t - last); last = t; if (++n < 60) requestAnimationFrame(tick); else resolve(frames); };
  requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) setTimeout(() => pane.dispatchEvent(new WheelEvent("wheel", { deltaY: i % 2 ? 120 : -120, clientX: 600, clientY: 400, bubbles: true })), i * 30);
}).then((frames) => { const s = [...frames].sort((a, b) => a - b); return { frames: s.length, p95: s[Math.floor(s.length * 0.95)], max: s[s.length - 1] }; })
```
  6. **The verdict.** S3 passes when warm `map:level` and `map:select` interactions and zoom frames all have a p95 ≤ 100 ms. Record every max as well. A cold first level, or the first search loading the embedding model, is reported apart and doesn't count.
